{"body":"## Summary\n\nFixes zombie sessions being re-condensed into every commit indefinitely.\n\n**Root cause:** After condensation, carry-forward sets `FilesTouched` with uncommitted files. On subsequent commits, `sessionHasNewContent` returns true (shadow branch has content), and the condensation handlers condense the session again without checking whether the committed files actually relate to the session. This cycle repeats on every commit.\n\n**Affected code paths:**\n- `HandleCondense` (ACTIVE/IDLE sessions) — skipped the overlap check entirely for ACTIVE sessions (`hasNew=true` unconditionally)\n- `HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched` (ENDED sessions) — had zero overlap checking, only checked `len(FilesTouched) > 0 && hasNew`\n\n**Impact:** In one real case, 6 zombie sessions accumulated over 11 days (Feb 8–19), polluting 12 checkpoints with 18 duplicate condensation events.\n\n**Fix:** Extract `shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck()` as a shared method that intersects session files with actually-committed files before calling `filesOverlapWithContent`. Both `HandleCondense` and `HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched` now use this method, ensuring ALL condensation paths verify file overlap.\n\n## Commits\n\n### 1. `bee7df26` — fix stale ACTIVE sessions being condensed into every commit\n- Add `filesTouchedBefore` field to `postCommitActionHandler`, populated from `state.FilesTouched` (IDLE/ENDED) or transcript extraction (ACTIVE)\n- Extract `shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck()` method: intersects `filesTouchedBefore` with `committedFileSet`, then calls `filesOverlapWithContent` on the intersection\n- Both `HandleCondense` and `HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched` now use this shared method\n- Add `TestPostCommit_StaleActiveSession_NotCondensed` regression test\n\n### 2. `744c4f3f` — fix ENDED sessions with carry-forward re-condensed into every commit\n- Apply the same overlap check to `HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched` (ENDED sessions), which previously had zero overlap checking\n- Add `TestPostCommit_EndedSessionCarryForward_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit` regression test\n- Fix `TestHandleTurnEnd_PartialFailure` to include `second.txt` in `FilesTouched` so the overlap check passes\n\n### 3. `014f53f2` — fix IDLE sessions with empty FilesTouched incorrectly condensed\n- Address review feedback: `shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck()` was fail-open when `filesTouchedBefore` was empty, causing IDLE sessions with no files (e.g., conversation-only) to be condensed into unrelated commits\n- Add `isActive` parameter: ACTIVE sessions fail-open (handles mid-turn multi-session commits), IDLE/ENDED sessions return false\n- Add `TestPostCommit_IdleSessionEmptyFilesTouched_NotCondensed` regression test\n\n## Test plan\n\n- [x] `TestPostCommit_StaleActiveSession_NotCondensed`\n- [x] `TestPostCommit_EndedSessionCarryForward_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit`\n- [x] `TestPostCommit_IdleSessionEmptyFilesTouched_NotCondensed`\n- [x] `TestPostCommit_ActiveSessionAlwaysCondenses` — ACTIVE sessions with no files still condense (multi-session)\n- [x] `TestHandleTurnEnd_PartialFailure` — updated for overlap check\n- [x] All existing PostCommit tests pass (OldIdleSession, OldEndedSession, CarryForward, etc.)\n- [x] Integration tests pass\n- [x] `mise run fmt && mise run lint` clean\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude diff --git a/cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/hooks.go b/cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/hooks.go
index 2b5da387b..49fccd2c4 100644
--- a/cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/hooks.go
+++ b/cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/hooks.go
@@ -455,6 +455,29 @@ func (env *TestEnv) GetSessionState(sessionID string) (*strategy.SessionState, e
 	return &state, nil
 }
 
+// WriteSessionState writes the session state for the given session ID.
+// This is useful for tests that need to manipulate session state directly.
+func (env *TestEnv) WriteSessionState(sessionID string, state *strategy.SessionState) error {
+	env.T.Helper()
+
+	stateDir := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "entire-sessions")
+	if err := os.MkdirAll(stateDir, 0o755); err != nil {
+		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session state dir: %w", err)
+	}
+
+	stateFile := filepath.Join(stateDir, sessionID+".json")
+
+	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(state, "", "  ")
+	if err != nil {
+		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal session state: %w", err)
+	}
+
+	if err := os.WriteFile(stateFile, data, 0o644); err != nil {
+		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write session state: %w", err)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
 // GeminiHookRunner executes Gemini CLI hooks in the test environment.
 type GeminiHookRunner struct {
 	RepoDir          string
diff --git a/cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/old_session_basecommit_test.go b/cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/old_session_basecommit_test.go
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..651fbb20e
--- /dev/null
+++ b/cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/old_session_basecommit_test.go
@@ -0,0 +1,291 @@
+//go:build integration
+
+package integration
+
+import (
+	"testing"
+
+	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session"
+	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy"
+)
+
+// TestOldIdleSession_BaseCommitNotUpdated verifies that when an old IDLE session
+// exists and a new ACTIVE session makes a commit, the old IDLE session's BaseCommit
+// is NOT updated to the new HEAD.
+//
+// This is a regression test for the bug where old sessions (IDLE/ENDED) would
+// have their BaseCommit incorrectly updated, causing them to be condensed on
+// future commits because their BaseCommit matched the new shadow branch.
+//
+// Scenario:
+// 1. Create an old session (session1), run full workflow, set to IDLE
+// 2. Make an unrelated commit to move HEAD forward (simulating time passing)
+// 3. Create a new session (session2), make changes
+// 4. Commit from session2
+// 5. Verify session1's BaseCommit was NOT updated
+func TestOldIdleSession_BaseCommitNotUpdated(t *testing.T) {
+	t.Parallel()
+	env := NewTestEnv(t)
+	defer env.Cleanup()
+
+	// ========================================
+	// Setup
+	// ========================================
+	env.InitRepo()
+	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
+	env.GitAdd("README.md")
+	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
+	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/test-base-commit")
+	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
+
+	// ========================================
+	// Phase 1: Create first session (will become IDLE)
+	// ========================================
+	t.Log("Phase 1: Creating first session that will become IDLE")
+
+	session1 := env.NewSession()
+	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session1.ID); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit for session1 failed: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	// Create a file and checkpoint
+	env.WriteFile("file1.txt", "content from session 1")
+	session1.CreateTranscript(
+		"Create file1",
+		[]FileChange{{Path: "file1.txt", Content: "content from session 1"}},
+	)
+	if err := env.SimulateStop(session1.ID, session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop for session1 failed: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	// Verify session1 is IDLE
+	state1, err := env.GetSessionState(session1.ID)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session1 failed: %v", err)
+	}
+	if state1.Phase != session.PhaseIdle {
+		t.Fatalf("Expected session1 to be IDLE, got %s", state1.Phase)
+	}
+
+	// Record session1's BaseCommit BEFORE the unrelated commit
+	session1OriginalBaseCommit := state1.BaseCommit
+	t.Logf("Session1 original BaseCommit: %s", session1OriginalBaseCommit[:7])
+
+	// ========================================
+	// Phase 2: Make an unrelated commit to move HEAD forward
+	// ========================================
+	t.Log("Phase 2: Making unrelated commit to move HEAD forward")
+
+	env.WriteFile("unrelated.txt", "unrelated file")
+	env.GitAdd("unrelated.txt")
+	env.GitCommit("Unrelated commit without checkpoint trailer")
+
+	newHeadAfterUnrelated := env.GetHeadHash()
+	t.Logf("HEAD after unrelated commit: %s", newHeadAfterUnrelated[:7])
+
+	// ========================================
+	// Phase 3: Create second session (ACTIVE)
+	// ========================================
+	t.Log("Phase 3: Creating second session")
+
+	session2 := env.NewSession()
+	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session2.ID); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit for session2 failed: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	// Create a file and checkpoint for session2
+	env.WriteFile("file2.txt", "content from session 2")
+	session2.CreateTranscript(
+		"Create file2",
+		[]FileChange{{Path: "file2.txt", Content: "content from session 2"}},
+	)
+	if err := env.SimulateStop(session2.ID, session2.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop for session2 failed: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	// Set session2 to ACTIVE (simulating agent mid-turn)
+	state2, err := env.GetSessionState(session2.ID)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session2 failed: %v", err)
+	}
+	state2.Phase = session.PhaseActive
+	if err := env.WriteSessionState(session2.ID, state2); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("WriteSessionState for session2 failed: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	// ========================================
+	// Phase 4: Commit from session2 (triggers PostCommit)
+	// ========================================
+	t.Log("Phase 4: Committing from session2")
+
+	env.GitAdd("file2.txt")
+	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Commit from session2", "file2.txt")
+
+	finalHead := env.GetHeadHash()
+	t.Logf("Final HEAD after session2 commit: %s", finalHead[:7])
+
+	// ========================================
+	// Phase 5: Verify session1's BaseCommit was NOT updated
+	// ========================================
+	t.Log("Phase 5: Verifying session1's BaseCommit was NOT updated")
+
+	state1After, err := env.GetSessionState(session1.ID)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session1 after commit failed: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	if state1After.BaseCommit != session1OriginalBaseCommit {
+		t.Errorf("OLD IDLE session's BaseCommit was incorrectly updated!\n"+
+			"Expected: %s (original)\n"+
+			"Got:      %s (new HEAD)",
+			session1OriginalBaseCommit[:7], state1After.BaseCommit[:7])
+	}
+
+	if state1After.BaseCommit == finalHead {
+		t.Errorf("OLD IDLE session's BaseCommit incorrectly matches new HEAD: %s", finalHead[:7])
+	}
+
+	// Session2's BaseCommit SHOULD be updated (it was the active session)
+	state2After, err := env.GetSessionState(session2.ID)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session2 after commit failed: %v", err)
+	}
+	if state2After.BaseCommit != finalHead {
+		t.Errorf("NEW ACTIVE session's BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD\n"+
+			"Expected: %s\n"+
+			"Got:      %s",
+			finalHead[:7], state2After.BaseCommit[:7])
+	}
+
+	t.Log("Test completed")
+}
+
+// TestOldEndedSession_BaseCommitNotUpdated verifies that when an old ENDED session
+// exists and a new ACTIVE session makes a commit, the old ENDED session's BaseCommit
+// is NOT updated to the new HEAD.
+func TestOldEndedSession_BaseCommitNotUpdated(t *testing.T) {
+	t.Parallel()
+	env := NewTestEnv(t)
+	defer env.Cleanup()
+
+	// ========================================
+	// Setup
+	// ========================================
+	env.InitRepo()
+	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
+	env.GitAdd("README.md")
+	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
+	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/test-ended-base-commit")
+	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
+
+	// ========================================
+	// Phase 1: Create first session and END it
+	// ========================================
+	t.Log("Phase 1: Creating first session that will be ENDED")
+
+	session1 := env.NewSession()
+	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session1.ID); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit for session1 failed: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	// Create a file and checkpoint
+	env.WriteFile("file1.txt", "content from session 1")
+	session1.CreateTranscript(
+		"Create file1",
+		[]FileChange{{Path: "file1.txt", Content: "content from session 1"}},
+	)
+	if err := env.SimulateStop(session1.ID, session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop for session1 failed: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	// Set session1 to ENDED with no new content (simulating already-condensed session)
+	state1, err := env.GetSessionState(session1.ID)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session1 failed: %v", err)
+	}
+	state1.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
+	// Mark transcript as fully condensed (no new content since last checkpoint).
+	// Set to a high value to ensure transcriptLines <= CheckpointTranscriptStart.
+	state1.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 1000
+	// Clear FilesTouched to simulate a session that was fully condensed
+	state1.FilesTouched = nil
+	if err := env.WriteSessionState(session1.ID, state1); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("WriteSessionState for session1 failed: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	// Record session1's BaseCommit BEFORE the unrelated commit
+	session1OriginalBaseCommit := state1.BaseCommit
+	t.Logf("Session1 original BaseCommit: %s", session1OriginalBaseCommit[:7])
+
+	// ========================================
+	// Phase 2: Make an unrelated commit to move HEAD forward
+	// ========================================
+	t.Log("Phase 2: Making unrelated commit to move HEAD forward")
+
+	env.WriteFile("unrelated.txt", "unrelated file")
+	env.GitAdd("unrelated.txt")
+	env.GitCommit("Unrelated commit without checkpoint trailer")
+
+	// ========================================
+	// Phase 3: Create second session (ACTIVE)
+	// ========================================
+	t.Log("Phase 3: Creating second session")
+
+	session2 := env.NewSession()
+	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session2.ID); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit for session2 failed: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	// Create a file and checkpoint for session2
+	env.WriteFile("file2.txt", "content from session 2")
+	session2.CreateTranscript(
+		"Create file2",
+		[]FileChange{{Path: "file2.txt", Content: "content from session 2"}},
+	)
+	if err := env.SimulateStop(session2.ID, session2.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop for session2 failed: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	// Set session2 to ACTIVE
+	state2, err := env.GetSessionState(session2.ID)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session2 failed: %v", err)
+	}
+	state2.Phase = session.PhaseActive
+	if err := env.WriteSessionState(session2.ID, state2); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("WriteSessionState for session2 failed: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	// ========================================
+	// Phase 4: Commit from session2
+	// ========================================
+	t.Log("Phase 4: Committing from session2")
+
+	env.GitAdd("file2.txt")
+	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Commit from session2", "file2.txt")
+
+	finalHead := env.GetHeadHash()
+
+	// ========================================
+	// Phase 5: Verify session1's BaseCommit was NOT updated
+	// ========================================
+	t.Log("Phase 5: Verifying session1's BaseCommit was NOT updated")
+
+	state1After, err := env.GetSessionState(session1.ID)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session1 after commit failed: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	if state1After.BaseCommit != session1OriginalBaseCommit {
+		t.Errorf("OLD ENDED session's BaseCommit was incorrectly updated!\n"+
+			"Expected: %s (original)\n"+
+			"Got:      %s (new HEAD)",
+			session1OriginalBaseCommit[:7], state1After.BaseCommit[:7])
+	}
+
+	if state1After.BaseCommit == finalHead {
+		t.Errorf("OLD ENDED session's BaseCommit incorrectly matches new HEAD: %s", finalHead[:7])
+	}
+
+	t.Log("Test completed")
+}

@@ -781,7 +781,15 @@ func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) condenseAndUpdateState(
 }
 
 // updateBaseCommitIfChanged updates BaseCommit to newHead if it changed.
+// Only updates ACTIVE sessions. IDLE/ENDED sessions should NOT have their
+// BaseCommit updated, as this would cause them to be incorrectly associated
+// with a new shadow branch and potentially condensed on future commits.
 func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx context.Context, state *SessionState, newHead string) {
+	// Only update ACTIVE sessions. IDLE/ENDED sessions are kept around for
+	// LastCheckpointID reuse and should not be advanced to HEAD.
+	if !state.Phase.IsActive() {
+		return
+	}
 	if state.BaseCommit != newHead {
 		state.BaseCommit = newHead
 		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: updated BaseCommit",

+// TestPostCommit_OldIdleSession_BaseCommitNotUpdated verifies that when an IDLE
+// session from a previous commit exists, and a NEW session makes a commit, the
+// old IDLE session's BaseCommit is NOT updated to the new HEAD.
+//
+// This is a regression test for the bug where old sessions (IDLE/ENDED) would
+// have their BaseCommit updated, causing them to be incorrectly condensed on
+// future commits because their BaseCommit matched the new shadow branch.
+func TestPostCommit_OldIdleSession_BaseCommitNotUpdated(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
+	t.Chdir(dir)
+
+	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
+
+	// --- Create an old IDLE session from a previous commit ---
+	oldSessionID := "old-idle-session"
+	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, oldSessionID)
+
+	oldState, err := s.loadSessionState(oldSessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	oldState.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
+	oldState.FilesTouched = []string{"old-file.txt"} // Has files touched (important for bug)
+	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(oldState))
+
+	// Record the old session's BaseCommit BEFORE the new commit
+	oldSessionOriginalBaseCommit := oldState.BaseCommit
+
+	// Create a commit to move HEAD forward (simulating old session was condensed)
+	wt, err := repo.Worktree()
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "unrelated.txt"), []byte("unrelated"), 0o644))
+	_, err = wt.Add("unrelated.txt")
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	_, err = wt.Commit("unrelated commit without trailer", &git.CommitOptions{
+		Author: &object.Signature{Name: "Test", Email: "test@test.com", When: time.Now()},
+	})
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	// --- Create a NEW ACTIVE session at the new HEAD ---
+	newSessionID := "new-active-session"
+	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, newSessionID)
+
+	newState, err := s.loadSessionState(newSessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	newState.Phase = session.PhaseActive
+	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(newState))
+
+	// --- Commit from the new session ---
+	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "a1b2c3d4e5f6")
+
+	// Get new HEAD for comparison
+	head, err := repo.Head()
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	newHead := head.Hash().String()
+
+	// Run PostCommit
+	err = s.PostCommit()
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	// --- Verify: old IDLE session's BaseCommit should NOT be updated ---
+	oldState, err = s.loadSessionState(oldSessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	assert.Equal(t, oldSessionOriginalBaseCommit, oldState.BaseCommit,
+		"OLD IDLE session's BaseCommit should NOT be updated when a different session commits")
+	assert.NotEqual(t, newHead, oldState.BaseCommit,
+		"OLD IDLE session's BaseCommit should NOT match new HEAD")
+
+	// New ACTIVE session's BaseCommit SHOULD be updated (it was condensed)
+	newState, err = s.loadSessionState(newSessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	assert.Equal(t, newHead, newState.BaseCommit,
+		"NEW ACTIVE session's BaseCommit should be updated after condensation")
+}
+

+	oldState.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
+	oldState.EndedAt = &now
+	oldState.FilesTouched = []string{"old-file.txt"} // Has files touched

+	// The transcript has 2 lines, so CheckpointTranscriptStart=2 means no new content
+	oldState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 2
+	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(oldState))
+
+	// Record the old session's BaseCommit BEFORE the new commit
+	oldSessionOriginalBaseCommit := oldState.BaseCommit
+
+	// Create a commit to move HEAD forward
+	wt, err := repo.Worktree()
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "unrelated.txt"), []byte("unrelated"), 0o644))
+	_, err = wt.Add("unrelated.txt")
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	_, err = wt.Commit("unrelated commit without trailer", &git.CommitOptions{
+		Author: &object.Signature{Name: "Test", Email: "test@test.com", When: time.Now()},
+	})
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	// --- Create a NEW ACTIVE session at the new HEAD ---
+	newSessionID := "new-active-session"
+	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, newSessionID)
+
+	newState, err := s.loadSessionState(newSessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	newState.Phase = session.PhaseActive
+	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(newState))
+
+	// --- Commit from the new session ---
+	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "b1c2d3e4f5a6")
+

+	head, err := repo.Head()

+

+	oldState, err = s.loadSessionState(oldSessionID)

+// shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck returns true if the session should be condensed
+// into this commit. Requires both that hasNew is true AND that the session's files
+// overlap with the committed files with matching content.
+//
+// This prevents stale sessions (ACTIVE sessions where the agent was killed, or
+// ENDED/IDLE sessions with carry-forward files) from being condensed into every
+// unrelated commit.
+//
+// filesTouchedBefore is populated from:
+//   - state.FilesTouched for IDLE/ENDED sessions (set via SaveStep/SaveTaskStep -> mergeFilesTouched)
+//   - transcript extraction for ACTIVE sessions when FilesTouched is empty
+//
+// When filesTouchedBefore is empty:
+//   - For ACTIVE sessions: fail-open (trust hasNew) because the agent may be
+//     mid-turn before any files are saved to state.
+//   - For IDLE/ENDED sessions: return false because there are no files to
+//     overlap with the commit.
+func (h *postCommitActionHandler) shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck(isActive bool) bool {
+	if !h.hasNew {
+		return false
+	}
+	if len(h.filesTouchedBefore) == 0 {
+		return isActive // ACTIVE: fail-open; IDLE/ENDED: no files = no overlap
+	}
+	// Only check files that were actually changed in this commit.
+	// Without this, files that exist in the tree but weren't changed
+	// would pass the "modified file" check in filesOverlapWithContent
+	// (because the file exists in the parent tree), causing stale
+	// sessions to be incorrectly condensed.
+	var committedTouchedFiles []string
+	for _, f := range h.filesTouchedBefore {
+		if _, ok := h.committedFileSet[f]; ok {
+			committedTouchedFiles = append(committedTouchedFiles, f)
+		}
+	}
+	if len(committedTouchedFiles) == 0 {
+		return false
+	}
+	return filesOverlapWithContent(h.repo, h.shadowBranchName, h.commit, committedTouchedFiles)
+}
+

> AGENT

Now I have a clear picture. Let me summarize the comparison:

## Summary: Two Different Issues (Same Symptom)

Both PRs address **stale sessions being condensed into every commit**, but they fix **different root causes**.

---

### PR 359 (Merged): BaseCommit Update Bug

**Root cause:** `updateBaseCommitIfChanged()` updated BaseCommit for ALL sessions, including IDLE/ENDED.

**Mechanism:**
1. Old IDLE session exists at commit A
2. New session commits → new HEAD is commit B
3. Old session's BaseCommit incorrectly updated to B
4. Old session now "associated" with the new shadow branch
5. Future commits see old session as having content → condense it again

**Fix:** Only update BaseCommit for ACTIVE sessions:
```go
if !state.Phase.IsActive() {
    return
}
```

---

### PR 418 (Current): Missing Overlap Check Bug

**Root cause:** After condensation, carry-forward sets `FilesTouched` with uncommitted files. Subsequent commits check `hasNew` (shadow branch has content) but **don't verify the committed files actually overlap with the session's files**.

**Mechanism:**
1. Session condenses, carry-forward sets `FilesTouched = ["uncommitted-file.txt"]`
2. User commits different files (e.g., `other-file.txt`)
3. `hasNew = true` (shadow branch still has uncommitted-file.txt content)
4. `HandleCondense` and `HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched` skip the overlap check
5. Session incorrectly condensed into unrelated commit
6. Repeat on every commit

**Affected code paths:**
- `HandleCondense` — skipped overlap check entirely for ACTIVE sessions
- `HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched` — had zero overlap checking for ENDED sessions

**Fix:** New `shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck()` method that:
1. Intersects session's `filesTouchedBefore` with `committedFileSet`
2. Only calls `filesOverlapWithContent` on the intersection
3. ACTIVE sessions fail-open if `filesTouchedBefore` is empty (mid-turn edge case)
4. IDLE/ENDED sessions return false if no overlap

---

### Relationship

These are **different bugs with the same symptom**. PR 359 was a prerequisite fix that stopped BaseCommit from being incorrectly updated, but **sessions with carry-forward files could still be condensed repeatedly** due to the missing overlap check. PR 418 closes that remaining gap.

The PR descriptions correctly identify them as separate issues - the current PR explicitly mentions the carry-forward scenario and the missing overlap checks in both `HandleCondense` and `HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched`.

+
+// TestPostCommit_EndedSessionCarryForward_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit verifies
+// that an ENDED session with carry-forward files is NOT condensed into a commit
+// that doesn't touch any of those files.
+//
+// This is the primary bug scenario: ENDED sessions go through HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched,
+// which previously only checked len(FilesTouched) > 0 && hasNew — no overlap check.
+// Carry-forward would set FilesTouched with remaining uncommitted files, and
+// sessionHasNewContent returned true because the shadow branch had content. This
+// caused ENDED sessions to be re-condensed into every subsequent commit indefinitely.
+func TestPostCommit_EndedSessionCarryForward_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
+	t.Chdir(dir)
+
+	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
+
+	// --- Create an ENDED session with carry-forward files ---
+	endedSessionID := "ended-carry-forward"
+	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, endedSessionID)
+
+	endedState, err := s.loadSessionState(endedSessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	now := time.Now()
+	endedState.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
+	endedState.EndedAt = &now
+	// Simulate carry-forward: session touched test.txt but it wasn't fully committed yet.
+	// CheckpointTranscriptStart=0 so sessionHasNewContent returns true (transcript grew).
+	endedState.FilesTouched = []string{"test.txt"}
+	endedState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0
+	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(endedState))
+
+	endedOriginalBaseCommit := endedState.BaseCommit
+	endedOriginalStepCount := endedState.StepCount
+
+	// Move HEAD forward with an unrelated commit (no trailer)
+	wt, err := repo.Worktree()
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "unrelated.txt"), []byte("unrelated work"), 0o644))
+	_, err = wt.Add("unrelated.txt")
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	_, err = wt.Commit("unrelated commit", &git.CommitOptions{
+		Author: &object.Signature{Name: "Test", Email: "test@test.com", When: time.Now()},
+	})
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	// --- Create a NEW ACTIVE session at the new HEAD ---
+	newSessionID := testNewActiveSessionID
+	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "new-feature.txt"), []byte("new feature content"), 0o644))
+
+	metadataDir := ".entire/metadata/" + newSessionID
+	metadataDirAbs := filepath.Join(dir, metadataDir)
+	require.NoError(t, os.MkdirAll(metadataDirAbs, 0o755))
+
+	transcript := `{"type":"human","message":{"content":"add new feature"}}
+{"type":"assistant","message":{"content":"adding new feature"}}
+`
+	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(
+		filepath.Join(metadataDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName),
+		[]byte(transcript), 0o644))
+
+	err = s.SaveStep(StepContext{
+		SessionID:      newSessionID,
+		ModifiedFiles:  []string{},
+		NewFiles:       []string{"new-feature.txt"},
+		DeletedFiles:   []string{},
+		MetadataDir:    metadataDir,
+		MetadataDirAbs: metadataDirAbs,
+		CommitMessage:  "Checkpoint: new feature",
+		AuthorName:     "Test",
+		AuthorEmail:    "test@test.com",
+	})
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	newState, err := s.loadSessionState(newSessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	newState.Phase = session.PhaseActive
+	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(newState))
+
+	// --- Commit ONLY new-feature.txt (not test.txt) with checkpoint trailer ---
+	wt, err = repo.Worktree()
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	_, err = wt.Add("new-feature.txt")
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	cpID := "ae1ae2ae3ae4"
+	commitMsg := "add new feature\n\n" + trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + cpID + "\n"
+	_, err = wt.Commit(commitMsg, &git.CommitOptions{
+		Author: &object.Signature{Name: "Test", Email: "test@test.com", When: time.Now()},
+	})
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	head, err := repo.Head()
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	newHead := head.Hash().String()
+
+	// Run PostCommit
+	err = s.PostCommit()
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	// --- Verify: ENDED session was NOT condensed ---
+	endedState, err = s.loadSessionState(endedSessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	// StepCount should be unchanged (not reset by condensation)
+	assert.Equal(t, endedOriginalStepCount, endedState.StepCount,
+		"ENDED session StepCount should NOT be reset (no condensation)")
+
+	// BaseCommit should NOT be updated for ENDED sessions (PR #359)
+	assert.Equal(t, endedOriginalBaseCommit, endedState.BaseCommit,
+		"ENDED session BaseCommit should NOT be updated")
+
+	// FilesTouched should still have the carry-forward files (not cleared by condensation)
+	assert.Equal(t, []string{"test.txt"}, endedState.FilesTouched,
+		"ENDED session FilesTouched should be preserved (carry-forward files not consumed)")
+
+	// Phase stays ENDED
+	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseEnded, endedState.Phase,
+		"ENDED session should remain ENDED")
+
+	// --- Verify: new ACTIVE session WAS condensed ---
+	newState, err = s.loadSessionState(newSessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	assert.Equal(t, 0, newState.StepCount,
+		"New ACTIVE session StepCount should be reset by condensation")
+	assert.Equal(t, newHead, newState.BaseCommit,
+		"New ACTIVE session BaseCommit should be updated after condensation")
+}
+
+// TestPostCommit_StaleActiveSession_NotCondensed verifies that a stale ACTIVE
+// session (agent killed without Stop hook) is NOT condensed into an unrelated
+// commit from a different session.
+//
+// Root cause: when an agent is killed without the Stop hook firing, its session
+// remains in ACTIVE phase permanently. Previously, PostCommit unconditionally
+// set hasNew=true for ACTIVE sessions and skipped the filesOverlapWithContent

    12→// TestOldIdleSession_BaseCommitNotUpdated verifies that when an old IDLE session
    13→// exists and a new ACTIVE session makes a commit, the old IDLE session's BaseCommit

    15→//
    16→// This is a regression test for the bug where old sessions (IDLE/ENDED) would
    17→// have their BaseCommit incorrectly updated, causing them to be condensed on

    19→//
    20→// Scenario:

    23→// 3. Create a new session (session2), make changes
    24→// 4. Commit from session2
    25→// 5. Verify session1's BaseCommit was NOT updated
    26→func TestOldIdleSession_BaseCommitNotUpdated(t *testing.T) {
    27→	t.Parallel()

    45→

    55→		[]FileChange{{Path: "file1.txt", Content: "content from session 1"}},

   163→// TestOldEndedSession_BaseCommitNotUpdated verifies that when an old ENDED session
   164→// exists and a new ACTIVE session makes a commit, the old ENDED session's BaseCommit
   165→// is NOT updated to the new HEAD.
   166→func TestOldEndedSession_BaseCommitNotUpdated(t *testing.T) {
   167→	t.Parallel()

   189→	}

   196→	)

   198→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop for session1 failed: %v", err)
   199→	}

   202→	state1, err := env.GetSessionState(session1.ID)
   203→	if err != nil {
   204→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session1 failed: %v", err)
   205→	}
   206→	state1.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
   207→	// Mark transcript as fully condensed (no new content since last checkpoint).
   208→	// Set to a high value to ensure transcriptLines <= CheckpointTranscriptStart.
   209→	state1.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 1000
   210→	// Clear FilesTouched to simulate a session that was fully condensed
   211→	state1.FilesTouched = nil
   212→	if err := env.WriteSessionState(session1.ID, state1); err != nil {
   213→		t.Fatalf("WriteSessionState for session1 failed: %v", err)
   214→	}

   226→	env.GitAdd("unrelated.txt")
   227→	env.GitCommit("Unrelated commit without checkpoint trailer")
   228→
   229→	// ========================================
   230→	// Phase 3: Create second session (ACTIVE)
   231→	// ========================================
   232→	t.Log("Phase 3: Creating second session")
   233→
   234→	session2 := env.NewSession()
   235→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session2.ID); err != nil {
   236→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit for session2 failed: %v", err)
   237→	}
   238→
   239→	// Create a file and checkpoint for session2
   240→	env.WriteFile("file2.txt", "content from session 2")

  "content": "//go:build integration\n\npackage integration\n\nimport (\n\t\"testing\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy\"\n)\n\n// TestCarryForward_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit verifies that a session with\n// carry-forward files is NOT condensed into a commit that doesn't touch those files.\n//\n// This is a regression test for the bug where sessions with carry-forward files\n// would be re-condensed into every subsequent commit indefinitely. The root cause\n// was that HandleCondense and HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched only checked hasNew\n// (shadow branch has content) but didn't verify that the committed files actually\n// overlapped with the session's FilesTouched.\n//\n// Scenario:\n// 1. Session 1 creates file1.txt and file2.txt\n// 2. User commits ONLY file1.txt (partial commit)\n// 3. Session 1 gets carry-forward: FilesTouched = [\"file2.txt\"]\n// 4. Session 1 ends\n// 5. New session 2 creates file3.txt (unrelated to session 1)\n// 6. Session 2 commits file3.txt\n// 7. Verify: Session 1 was NOT condensed (FilesTouched preserved, StepCount unchanged)\nfunc TestCarryForward_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\tenv := NewTestEnv(t)\n\tdefer env.Cleanup()\n\n\t// ========================================\n\t// Setup\n\t// ========================================\n\tenv.InitRepo()\n\tenv.WriteFile(\"README.md\", \"# Test Repository\")\n\tenv.GitAdd(\"README.md\")\n\tenv.GitCommit(\"Initial commit\")\n\tenv.GitCheckoutNewBranch(\"feature/carry-forward-test\")\n\tenv.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)\n\n\t// ========================================\n\t// Phase 1: Session 1 creates multiple files\n\t// ========================================\n\tt.Log(\"Phase 1: Session 1 creates file1.txt and file2.txt\")\n\n\tsession1 := env.NewSession()\n\tif err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session1.ID); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"SimulateUserPromptSubmit for session1 failed: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Create two files\n\tenv.WriteFile(\"file1.txt\", \"content from session 1 - file 1\")\n\tenv.WriteFile(\"file2.txt\", \"content from session 1 - file 2\")\n\tsession1.CreateTranscript(\n\t\t\"Create file1 and file2\",\n\t\t[]FileChange{\n\t\t\t{Path: \"file1.txt\", Content: \"content from session 1 - file 1\"},\n\t\t\t{Path: \"file2.txt\", Content: \"content from session 1 - file 2\"},\n\t\t},\n\t)\n\tif err := env.SimulateStop(session1.ID, session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"SimulateStop for session1 failed: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify session1 is IDLE with both files in FilesTouched\n\tstate1, err := env.GetSessionState(session1.ID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"GetSessionState for session1 failed: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif state1.Phase != session.PhaseIdle {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Expected session1 to be IDLE, got %s\", state1.Phase)\n\t}\n\tt.Logf(\"Session1 FilesTouched before partial commit: %v\", state1.FilesTouched)\n\n\t// ========================================\n\t// Phase 2: Partial commit - only file1.txt\n\t// ========================================\n\tt.Log(\"Phase 2: Committing only file1.txt (partial commit)\")\n\n\tenv.GitAdd(\"file1.txt\")\n\t// Note: file2.txt is NOT staged\n\tenv.GitCommitWithShadowHooks(\"Partial commit: only file1\", \"file1.txt\")\n\n\t// Verify carry-forward: file2.txt should remain in FilesTouched\n\tstate1, err = env.GetSessionState(session1.ID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"GetSessionState for session1 after partial commit failed: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tt.Logf(\"Session1 FilesTouched after partial commit: %v\", state1.FilesTouched)\n\n\t// file2.txt should be carried forward (not committed yet)\n\thasFile2 := false\n\tfor _, f := range state1.FilesTouched {\n\t\tif f == \"file2.txt\" {\n\t\t\thasFile2 = true\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif !hasFile2 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Expected file2.txt to be carried forward in FilesTouched, got: %v\", state1.FilesTouched)\n\t}\n\n\t// Record state for later comparison\n\tsession1StepCountAfterPartial := state1.StepCount\n\tsession1BaseCommitAfterPartial := state1.BaseCommit\n\n\t// ========================================\n\t// Phase 3: End session 1 (simulating user closes agent)\n\t// ========================================\n\tt.Log(\"Phase 3: Ending session 1\")\n\n\tstate1.Phase = session.PhaseEnded\n\tif err := env.WriteSessionState(session1.ID, state1); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"WriteSessionState for session1 failed: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// ========================================\n\t// Phase 4: New session 2 creates unrelated file\n\t// ========================================\n\tt.Log(\"Phase 4: Session 2 creates file3.txt (unrelated to session 1)\")\n\n\tsession2 := env.NewSession()\n\tif err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session2.ID); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"SimulateUserPromptSubmit for session2 failed: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tenv.WriteFile(\"file3.txt\", \"content from session 2\")\n\tsession2.CreateTranscript(\n\t\t\"Create file3\",\n\t\t[]FileChange{{Path: \"file3.txt\", Content: \"content from session 2\"}},\n\t)\n\tif err := env.SimulateStop(session2.ID, session2.TranscriptPath); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"SimulateStop for session2 failed: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Set session2 to ACTIVE (simulating mid-turn commit)\n\tstate2, err := env.GetSessionState(session2.ID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"GetSessionState for session2 failed: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tstate2.Phase = session.PhaseActive\n\tif err := env.WriteSessionState(session2.ID, state2); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"WriteSessionState for session2 failed: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// ========================================\n\t// Phase 5: Commit file3.txt (unrelated to session 1's files)\n\t// ========================================\n\tt.Log(\"Phase 5: Committing file3.txt from session 2\")\n\n\tenv.GitAdd(\"file3.txt\")\n\tenv.GitCommitWithShadowHooks(\"Add file3 from session 2\", \"file3.txt\")\n\n\tfinalHead := env.GetHeadHash()\n\tt.Logf(\"Final HEAD: %s\", finalHead[:7])\n\n\t// ========================================\n\t// Phase 6: Verify session 1 was NOT condensed\n\t// ========================================\n\tt.Log(\"Phase 6: Verifying session 1 was NOT condensed into unrelated commit\")\n\n\tstate1After, err := env.GetSessionState(session1.ID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"GetSessionState for session1 after session2 commit failed: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// StepCount should be unchanged (condensation resets it)\n\tif state1After.StepCount != session1StepCountAfterPartial {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Session 1 StepCount changed! Expected %d, got %d (was incorrectly condensed)\",\n\t\t\tsession1StepCountAfterPartial, state1After.StepCount)\n\t}\n\n\t// BaseCommit should be unchanged (ENDED sessions don't get BaseCommit updated)\n\tif state1After.BaseCommit != session1BaseCommitAfterPartial {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Session 1 BaseCommit changed! Expected %s, got %s\",\n\t\t\tsession1BaseCommitAfterPartial[:7], state1After.BaseCommit[:7])\n\t}\n\n\t// FilesTouched should still have file2.txt (not cleared by condensation)\n\thasFile2After := false\n\tfor _, f := range state1After.FilesTouched {\n\t\tif f == \"file2.txt\" {\n\t\t\thasFile2After = true\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif !hasFile2After {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Session 1 FilesTouched was cleared! Expected %s, got %s\",\n\t\t\tfinalHead[:7], state2After.BaseCommit[:7])\n\t}\n\n\tt.Log(\"Test completed successfully\")\n}\n\n// TestStaleActiveSession_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit verifies that a stale\n// ACTIVE session (agent killed without Stop hook) is NOT condensed into an\n// unrelated commit from a different session.\n//\n// This is a regression test for the bug where ACTIVE sessions would always be\n// condensed (hasNew=true unconditionally) without checking file overlap.\n//\n// Scenario:\n// 1. One test failed. The issue is that for ACTIVE sessions (even stale ones), `updateBaseCommitIfChanged` still updates the BaseCommit - that's expected behavior. The key fix in PR 418 is the **overlap check** that prevents condensation, not the BaseCommit update.

Let me fix the test - the important assertions are that StepCount and FilesTouched are unchanged (no condensation), not the BaseCommit.

> AGENT

All tests pass. Here's a summary of the integration tests I added:

## New Integration Tests

Created `carry_forward_overlap_test.go` with three tests covering the scenarios fixed in PR 418:

### 1. `TestCarryForward_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit`
Tests the primary bug scenario:
- Session 1 creates `file1.txt` and `file2.txt`
- User commits only `file1.txt` (partial commit)
- Session 1 gets carry-forward: `FilesTouched = ["file2.txt"]`
- Session 1 ends
- New session 2 commits `file3.txt` (unrelated)
- **Verifies:** Session 1 was NOT condensed (FilesTouched preserved, StepCount unchanged)

### 2. `TestStaleActiveSession_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit`
Tests the agent-crash scenario:
- Session 1 creates `file1.txt`, agent is killed (stays ACTIVE without Stop hook)
- Session 2 creates and commits `file2.txt` (unrelated)
- **Verifies:** Stale session 1 was NOT condensed

### 3. `TestIdleSessionEmptyFilesTouched_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit`
Tests the conversation-only scenario:
- Session 1 has a conversation but doesn't modify any files
- Session 2 creates and commits `file1.txt`
- **Verifies:** Session 1 (conversation-only, empty FilesTouched) was NOT condensed

These integration tests complement the unit tests already in the PR and provide end-to-end verification through the full hook simulation flow.

> DEVELOPER

And this scneario? 

  Mechanism:
  1. Session condenses, carry-forward sets FilesTouched = ["uncommitted-file.txt"]
  2. User commits different files (e.g., other-file.txt)
  3. hasNew = true (shadow branch still has uncommitted-file.txt content)
  4. HandleCondense and HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched skip the overlap check
  5. Session incorrectly condensed into unrelated commit
  6. Repeat on every commit

Make 3 unrelated commits (file3.txt, file4.txt, file5.txt)\n// 5. Verify: Session 1 was NOT condensed into ANY of those commits\n// 6. Finally commit file2.txt\n// 7. Verify: Session 1 IS condensed (carry-forward consumed)\nfunc TestCarryForward_NotCondensedIntoMultipleUnrelatedCommits(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\tenv := NewTestEnv(t)\n\tdefer env.Cleanup()\n\n\t// ========================================\n\t// Setup\n\t// ========================================\n\tenv.InitRepo()\n\tenv.WriteFile(\"README.md\", \"# Test Repository\")\n\tenv.GitAdd(\"README.md\")\n\tenv.GitCommit(\"Initial commit\")\n\tenv.GitCheckoutNewBranch(\"feature/repeat-condensation-test\")\n\tenv.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)\n\n\t// ========================================\n\t// Phase 1: Session 1 creates multiple files\n\t// ========================================\n\tt.Log(\"Phase 1: Session 1 creates file1.txt and file2.txt\")\n\n\tsession1 := env.NewSession()\n\tif err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session1.ID); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"SimulateUserPromptSubmit for session1 failed: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tenv.WriteFile(\"file1.txt\", \"content from session 1 - file 1\")\n\tenv.WriteFile(\"file2.txt\", \"content from session 1 - file 2\")\n\tsession1.CreateTranscript(\n\t\t\"Create file1 and file2\",\n\t\t[]FileChange{\n\t\t\t{Path: \"file1.txt\", Content: \"content from session 1 - file 1\"},\n\t\t\t{Path: \"file2.txt\", Content: \"content from session 1 - file 2\"},\n\t\t},\n\t)\n\tif err := env.SimulateStop(session1.ID, session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"SimulateStop for session1 failed: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// ========================================\n\t// Phase 2: Partial commit - only file1.txt\n\t// ========================================\n\tt.Log(\"Phase 2: Committing only file1.txt (partial commit)\")\n\n\tenv.GitAdd(\"file1.txt\")\n\tenv.GitCommitWithShadowHooks(\"Partial commit: only file1\", \"file1.txt\")\n\n\t// Verify carry-forward\n\tstate1, err := env.GetSessionState(session1.ID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"GetSessionState for session1 after partial commit failed: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tt.Logf(\"Session1 FilesTouched after partial commit: %v\", state1.FilesTouched)\n\n\t// End session 1\n\tstate1.Phase = session.PhaseEnded\n\tif err := env.WriteSessionState(session1.ID, state1); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"WriteSessionState for session1 failed: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Record initial state for comparison\n\tsession1InitialStepCount := state1.StepCount\n\n\t// ========================================\n\t// Phase 3: Make multiple unrelated commits\n\t// ========================================\n\tunrelatedFiles := []string{\"file3.txt\", \"file4.txt\", \"file5.txt\"}\n\n\tfor i, fileName := range unrelatedFiles {\n\t\tt.Logf(\"Phase 3.%d: Making unrelated commit with %s\", i+1, fileName)\n\n\t\tenv.WriteFile(fileName, \"unrelated content \"+fileName)\n\t\tenv.GitAdd(fileName)\n\t\tenv.GitCommitWithShadowHooks(\"Add \"+fileName, fileName)\n\n\t\t// Verify session 1 was NOT condensed after each commit\n\t\tstate1, err = env.GetSessionState(session1.ID)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"GetSessionState for session1 after commit %d failed: %v\", i+1, err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif state1.StepCount != session1InitialStepCount {\n\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"After commit %d (%s): Session 1 StepCount changed from %d to %d (incorrectly condensed!)\",\n\t\t\t\ti+1, fileName, session1InitialStepCount, state1.StepCount)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// FilesTouched should still have file2.txt\n\t\thasFile2 := false\n\t\tfor _, f := range state1.FilesTouched {\n\t\t\tif f == \"file2.txt\" {\n\t\t\t\thasFile2 = true\n\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif !hasFile2 {\n\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"After commit %d (%s): Session 1 FilesTouched was cleared! ### `carry_forward_overlap_test.go`

| Test | Scenario | Verifies |
|------|----------|----------|

| **`TestCarryForward_NotCondensedIntoMultipleUnrelatedCommits`** | Session with carry-forward → **3 unrelated commits** → finally commit the carry-forward file | Session NOT condensed into any of the 3 unrelated commits, but IS condensed when its file is committed |
| `TestStaleActiveSession_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit` | Agent crash (stays ACTIVE) → unrelated commit | Stale session NOT condensed |
| `TestIdleSessionEmptyFilesTouched_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit` | Conversation-only session (no files) → unrelated commit | Empty-files session NOT condensed |

The new `TestCarryForward_NotCondensedIntoMultipleUnrelatedCommits` test directly covers the "repeat on every commit" bug mechanism you described:

```
1. Session condenses, carry-forward sets FilesTouched = ["file2.txt"]
2. User commits file3.txt → NOT condensed ✓
3. User commits file4.txt → NOT condensed ✓  
4. User commits file5.txt → NOT condensed ✓
5. User commits file2.txt → IS condensed ✓ (carry-forward consumed)
```

Good question - let me trace through the code for that scenario.

**Scenario:**
1. Session 1: creates file1.txt, file2.txt
2. Commit file1.txt → Session 1 carry-forward: `FilesTouched=["file2.txt"]`, ends
3. Commit file3.txt (unrelated) → Session 1 NOT condensed
4. Commit file4.txt (unrelated) → Session 1 NOT condensed
5. **Session 2 starts, creates file6.txt**
6. Commit file6.txt

**Code flow in `PostCommit()` when file6.txt is committed:**

```go
// PostCommit iterates over ALL session states
states, err := s.loadAllSessionStates()
for _, state := range states {
    // Process Session 1 (ENDED) and Session 2 (ACTIVE)
}
```

**For Session 1 (ENDED, FilesTouched=["file2.txt"]):**
```go
// HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched is called
shouldCondense := len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 && h.shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck(false)

// shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck:
// - filesTouchedBefore = ["file2.txt"]
// - committedFileSet = {"file6.txt"}
// - intersection = [] (empty)
// - returns false
```
→ Session 1 NOT condensed ✓

**For Session 2 (ACTIVE, FilesTouched=["file6.txt"]):**
```go
// HandleCondense is called

// shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck:
// - filesTouchedBefore = ["file6.txt"]
// - committedFileSet = {"file6.txt"}
// - intersection = ["file6.txt"]
// - filesOverlapWithContent returns true
// - returns true
```
→ Session 2 IS condensed ✓

Expected %s, got %s\",\n\t\t\tfinalHead[:7], state2After.BaseCommit[:7])\n\t}\n\n\tt.Log(\"Test completed successfully - old session with carry-forward NOT condensed into new session's commit\")\n}\n\n// TestStaleActiveSession_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit verifies that a stale\n// ACTIVE session (agent killed without Stop hook) is NOT condensed into an\n// unrelated commit from a different session."
}

> AGENT

All tests pass. Here's the complete verified behavior:

## Complete Scenario Flow

```
1. Session 1: creates file1.txt, file2.txt
2. Commit file1.txt (partial) → carry-forward: FilesTouched=["file2.txt"]
3. Session 1 ends
4. Commit file3.txt (unrelated) → Session 1 NOT condensed ✓
5. Commit file4.txt (unrelated) → Session 1 NOT condensed ✓
6. Session 2 starts, creates file6.txt
7. Commit file6.txt (Session 2) → Session 1 NOT condensed ✓, Session 2 IS condensed ✓
8. Commit file2.txt → Session 1 IS condensed ✓ (carry-forward consumed)
```

The fix in PR 418 ensures:
- Sessions with carry-forward files are **only** condensed when their actual files are committed
- They are **not** condensed into unrelated commits (the bug that caused repeated condensation)
- Multiple sessions can coexist without interfering with each other's condensation

> DEVELOPER

is there an end to this? like when would the code stop to carry-forward session 1?

> AGENT

Great question. Let me trace through what happens to Session 1's state in different scenarios:

## When Session 1 WILL be cleaned up (condensed)

**Scenario: file2.txt is committed with matching content**
```
1. Session 1 has carry-forward: FilesTouched=["file2.txt"]
2. User commits file2.txt (with Session 1's content)
3. PostCommit: shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck passes (file in commit + content matches shadow)
4. Session 1 condensed → FilesTouched cleared, shadow branch deleted
```

## When Session 1 will persist FOREVER

**Scenario A: file2.txt is never committed**
```
1. Session 1 has carry-forward: FilesTouched=["file2.txt"]
2. User never commits file2.txt
3. Session 1 state persists in .git/entire-sessions/ indefinitely
```

**Scenario B: file2.txt is discarded**
```
1. Session 1 has carry-forward: FilesTouched=["file2.txt"]
2. User runs: git checkout -- file2.txt (discards changes)
3. Session 1 state still persists (no cleanup mechanism)
```

**Scenario C: New session modifies file2.txt differently**
```
1. Session 1 has carry-forward: FilesTouched=["file2.txt"] (content: "old")
2. Session 2 modifies file2.txt (content: "new")
3. User commits file2.txt
4. PostCommit: 
   - Session 1: filesOverlapWithContent checks shadow branch content vs committed content
   - Content doesn't match → Session 1 NOT condensed
   - Session 2: Content matches → Session 2 IS condensed
5. Session 1 persists forever with stale carry-forward
```

## Current behavior: No automatic cleanup

Looking at the code, there's no mechanism to clean up stale sessions:

```go
// PostCommit iterates over ALL session states every time
states, err := s.loadAllSessionStates()
for _, state := range states {
    // Process each session...
}
```

This means:
- Session state files accumulate in `.git/entire-sessions/`
- Every `PostCommit` iterates over all sessions (including stale ones)
- Dead sessions with unrecoverable carry-forward never get cleaned up

## This seems like a potential issue

Should there be cleanup logic for sessions where:
1. The carry-forward files no longer exist in the working directory?
2. The carry-forward files exist but content differs from shadow branch (user overwrote them)?
3. The session is older than X days?

Want me to investigate adding a cleanup mechanism, or is this acceptable for now?

> DEVELOPER

I feel the main idea for carry-forward was to support stashing changes and applying them later with potentially running a session in between. But not sure if "X days" work since you could just take a weekend brake and continue (but yeah we could do 4 days then) but also 4 days in a regular week is a lot of time with a lot of different sessions in between. Do you have other ideas?

> AGENT

Good point about the stash workflow. Let me think of alternatives:

## Option 1: Content-based cleanup (automatic)

If the carry-forward files in the working directory no longer match the shadow branch content, the session can **never** be condensed. We could detect this and clean up automatically.

```go
// In PostCommit, for ENDED sessions with carry-forward:
for _, file := range state.FilesTouched {
    workingContent := readWorkingTreeFile(file)
    shadowContent := readShadowBranchFile(shadowBranch, file)
    
    if workingContent != shadowContent {
        // File was modified/deleted/overwritten
        // This session can never be condensed → clean up
    }
}
```

**Problem:** Doesn't handle stash - working tree won't match but stash does.

## Option 2: Git status-based cleanup (automatic)

If carry-forward files are "clean" in git status (not modified, not untracked), the session is orphaned:

```go
// If file is NOT in git status (clean) but IS in FilesTouched:
// → File was committed differently or discarded → clean up
```

**Problem:** Same stash issue.

## Option 3: Stash-aware cleanup

Check both working tree AND stash entries for matching content. Only clean up if content doesn't match anywhere.

**Problem:** Complex, and what about other refs the user might have?

## Option 4: User-initiated cleanup

Add commands:
- `entire sessions` - List sessions, their carry-forward files, age
- `entire cleanup` - Interactively clean up stale sessions

**Pros:** User stays in control, no accidental cleanup
**Cons:** Manual, sessions accumulate if user doesn't run it

## Option 5: Hybrid (my recommendation)

1. **Automatic cleanup** for "definitely orphaned" sessions:
   - Carry-forward file doesn't exist AND isn't in stash
   - Or: file exists but content differs from shadow branch AND isn't in stash

2. **User commands** for everything else:
   - `entire sessions` to see what's lingering
   - `entire cleanup` for manual control

This handles the common cases automatically while preserving the stash workflow.

---

What do you think? The hybrid approach seems safest - we auto-clean obvious orphans but don't touch anything ambiguous.

> DEVELOPER

question: the carry-forward in the shadow branch only works in a linear history, right? like I keep commiting on top of each other in a branch or something, once I switch branches or merge in remote changes, the shadowbranch is truly lost

> AGENT

Let me trace through what happens with branch switches:

## Shadow Branch Persistence

The shadow branch itself (`entire/<BaseCommit[:7]>-<worktreeHash>`) is just a git ref - it persists in `.git/refs/heads/entire/` regardless of what branch you're on. It doesn't get deleted when you switch branches.

## The Issue: BaseCommit is Frozen

For ENDED sessions (per PR #359), `BaseCommit` is never updated. So:

```
1. Session 1 at commit A, creates file1.txt, file2.txt
2. Partial commit file1.txt → new HEAD is B
3. Session 1 ENDED, carry-forward: FilesTouched=["file2.txt"]
   - BaseCommit = B (updated during condensation while IDLE→IDLE)
   - Shadow branch: entire/<B[:7]>-<worktreeHash>

4. User switches to different branch (commit C)
5. User does work, commits, merges, etc.
6. User switches back, commits file2.txt
```

At step 6, PostCommit:
- Looks for shadow branch `entire/<B[:7]>-<worktreeHash>` ✓ (still exists)
- Checks `filesOverlapWithContent` comparing shadow content vs committed content

## Where It Breaks

**Scenario: Content still matches** → Works fine
```
- Shadow has file2.txt with content "X"
- User commits file2.txt with content "X"
- Content matches → Session 1 condensed ✓
```

**Scenario: Content diverged** → Orphaned forever
```
- Shadow has file2.txt with content "X"
- User rebased/merged and file2.txt now has content "Y"
- User commits file2.txt with content "Y"
- Content doesn't match → Session 1 NOT condensed
- Session 1 persists forever with stale carry-forward
```

## You're Right

The carry-forward mechanism effectively assumes:
1. **Linear-ish history** - no rebases/merges that modify the carry-forward files
2. **Content preservation** - the files aren't modified by other means

Once the content diverges, the shadow branch becomes "truly lost" in the sense that it can never be matched to any future commit.

## This Reinforces the Cleanup Need

This is another case where sessions become orphaned and need cleanup. The "content-based cleanup" approach would catch this:

```go
// If shadow branch content doesn't match working tree AND isn't in stash:
// → Session is orphaned, clean it up
```

Want me to look into implementing a cleanup mechanism that handles both scenarios (discarded files AND diverged content)?

   488→func (h *postCommitActionHandler) HandleCondense(state *session.State) error {
   489→	shouldCondense := h.shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck(state.Phase.IsActive())
   490→

   492→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),

   501→	} else {

   503→	}
   504→	return nil
   505→}
   506→
   507→func (h *postCommitActionHandler) HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched(state *session.State) error {
   508→	shouldCondense := len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 && h.shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck(state.Phase.IsActive())
   509→
   510→	logging.Debug(h.logCtx, "post-commit: HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched decision",
   511→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   512→		slog.String("phase", string(state.Phase)),
   513→		slog.Bool("has_new", h.hasNew),
   514→		slog.Int("files_touched", len(state.FilesTouched)),
   515→		slog.Bool("should_condense", shouldCondense),
   516→		slog.String("shadow_branch", h.shadowBranchName),
   517→	)
   518→
   519→	if shouldCondense {
   520→		h.condensed = h.s.condenseAndUpdateState(h.logCtx, h.repo, h.checkpointID, state, h.head, h.shadowBranchName, h.shadowBranchesToDelete, h.committedFileSet)
   521→	} else {
   522→		h.s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(h.logCtx, state, h.newHead)
   523→	}
   524→	return nil
   525→}
   526→
   527→// shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck returns true if the session should be condensed
   528→// into this commit. Requires both that hasNew is true AND that the session's files
   529→// overlap with the committed files with matching content.
   530→//
   531→// This prevents stale sessions (ACTIVE sessions where the agent was killed, or
   532→// ENDED/IDLE sessions with carry-forward files) from being condensed into every
   533→// unrelated commit.
   534→//
   535→// filesTouchedBefore is populated from:
   536→//   - state.FilesTouched for IDLE/ENDED sessions (set via SaveStep/SaveTaskStep -> mergeFilesTouched)
   537→//   - transcript extraction for ACTIVE sessions when FilesTouched is empty
   538→//
   539→// When filesTouchedBefore is empty:
   540→//   - For ACTIVE sessions: fail-open (trust hasNew) because the agent may be
   541→//     mid-turn before any files are saved to state.
   542→//   - For IDLE/ENDED sessions: return false because there are no files to
   543→//     overlap with the commit.
   544→func (h *postCommitActionHandler) shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck(isActive bool) bool {
   545→	if !h.hasNew {
   546→		return false
   547→	}
   548→	if len(h.filesTouchedBefore) == 0 {
   549→		return isActive // ACTIVE: fail-open; IDLE/ENDED: no files = no overlap
   550→	}
   551→	// Only check files that were actually changed in this commit.
   552→	// Without this, files that exist in the tree but weren't changed
   553→	// would pass the "modified file" check in filesOverlapWithContent
   554→	// (because the file exists in the parent tree), causing stale
   555→	// sessions to be incorrectly condensed.
   556→	var committedTouchedFiles []string
   557→	for _, f := range h.filesTouchedBefore {
   558→		if _, ok := h.committedFileSet[f]; ok {
   559→			committedTouchedFiles = append(committedTouchedFiles, f)
   560→		}
   561→	}
   562→	if len(committedTouchedFiles) == 0 {
   563→		return false
   564→	}
   565→	return filesOverlapWithContent(h.repo, h.shadowBranchName, h.commit, committedTouchedFiles)
   566→}
   567→
   568→func (h *postCommitActionHandler) HandleDiscardIfNoFiles(state *session.State) error {
   569→	if len(state.FilesTouched) == 0 {
   570→		logging.Debug(h.logCtx, "post-commit: skipping empty ended session (no files to condense)",
   571→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   572→		)
   573→	}
   574→	h.s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(h.logCtx, state, h.newHead)

**Integration tests provide coverage but don't trigger the exact bug scenario** because:
- The bug requires `hasNew = true` (transcript grew since last checkpoint)
- The integration test hook flow properly updates `CheckpointTranscriptStart` during condensation
- So `hasNew` becomes false naturally in subsequent commits

The **unit tests** directly manipulate `CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0` to force `hasNew = true`, which triggers the bug.

**Summary:**
- Unit tests in `phase_postcommit_test.go` catch the bug ✓
- Integration tests in `carry_forward_overlap_test.go` provide end-to-end coverage ✓
- Both test suites pass with the fix ✓

can you explain this more: 

  Integration tests provide coverage but don't trigger the exact bug scenario because:
  - The bug requires hasNew = true (transcript grew since last checkpoint)
  - The integration test hook flow properly updates CheckpointTranscriptStart during condensation
  - So hasNew becomes false naturally in subsequent commits

> AGENT

Let me trace through the exact flow:

## How `hasNew` is determined

```go
func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) sessionHasNewContent(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) (bool, error) {
    // Get transcript from shadow branch
    transcriptLines := countLinesInShadowBranch(shadowBranch, "full.jsonl")
    
    // Compare to last checkpoint position
    return transcriptLines > state.CheckpointTranscriptStart, nil
}
```

So `hasNew = true` when: **transcript lines > CheckpointTranscriptStart**

## Integration test flow

```
1. Session 1 creates file1.txt, file2.txt
   - Transcript: 2 lines (human + assistant)
   - Stop hook fires → shadow branch created with transcript
   - CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0 (not yet condensed)

2. Partial commit (file1.txt only)
   - PostCommit runs
   - hasNew = (2 > 0) = TRUE
   - Session 1 condensed ✓
   - CheckpointTranscriptStart = 2  ← UPDATED during condensation
   - Carry-forward: FilesTouched = ["file2.txt"]
   - Session set to ENDED

3. Unrelated commit (file3.txt)
   - PostCommit runs
   - hasNew = (2 > 2) = FALSE  ← No new transcript content!
   - shouldCondense = FilesTouched.len > 0 && hasNew = true && false = FALSE
   - Session 1 NOT condensed (but for the wrong reason)
```

## Why the bug doesn't manifest

The buggy code was:
```go
shouldCondense := len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 && h.hasNew
```

In the integration test:
- `len(state.FilesTouched) > 0` = TRUE (file2.txt)
- `h.hasNew` = **FALSE** (transcript hasn't grown)
- Result: `shouldCondense = FALSE`

So the session isn't condensed, but **only because `hasNew` is false**, not because of the overlap check.

## The actual bug scenario

The bug manifests when `hasNew` is TRUE but files don't overlap:

```
1. Session with carry-forward, CheckpointTranscriptStart somehow = 0
   (due to bug, race condition, or state corruption)

2. Unrelated commit
   - hasNew = (2 > 0) = TRUE
   - shouldCondense = true && true = TRUE
   - Session incorrectly condensed into unrelated commit!
```

The unit test forces this by manually setting `CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0`:
```go
endedState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0  // Force hasNew=true
```

## In real-world usage

When would `hasNew` be true for a stale session?

1. **Agent resumes and does more work** - transcript grows, `hasNew` becomes true
2. **Bug in CheckpointTranscriptStart update** - counter not updated properly
3. **State file corruption/reset** - counter reset to 0

The fix ensures that even when `hasNew` is true, we still check if the **committed files overlap with the session's files** before condensing.

> AGENT

You're right to be puzzled. Let me trace through more carefully - I think I see the issue now.

## The Problem with My Integration Tests

In `TestStaleActiveSession_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit`:

```go
// Step 1: Session 1 starts
env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session1.ID)

// Step 2: Create file and call Stop hook
env.WriteFile("file1.txt", ...)
session1.CreateTranscript(...)
env.SimulateStop(session1.ID, ...)  // ← THIS UPDATES CheckpointTranscriptStart!

// Step 3: Manually set back to ACTIVE
state1.Phase = session.PhaseActive  // ← But CheckpointTranscriptStart is already updated!
env.WriteSessionState(session1.ID, state1)
```

The Stop hook updates `CheckpointTranscriptStart` to the current transcript length. So when I manually set the phase back to ACTIVE, the session has:
- `Phase = ACTIVE`
- `CheckpointTranscriptStart = 2` (already updated by Stop)
- `transcriptLines = 2`
- `hasNew = (2 > 2) = FALSE`

## A Real Stale ACTIVE Session

A truly stale ACTIVE session (agent killed mid-turn) would have:
- `Phase = ACTIVE`
- `CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0` (never updated - Stop never ran)
- `transcriptLines = 2`
- `hasNew = (2 > 0) = TRUE`

## So the Integration Tests Are Wrong

My tests don't accurately simulate the bug because they call `SimulateStop` which updates `CheckpointTranscriptStart`. The session appears stale (ACTIVE phase) but has the internal state of a properly-stopped session.

> AGENT

I see the issue. Looking at the output:
```
Session1: Created shadow branch 'entire/676b805-e3b0c4'
Session2: Committed changes to shadow branch 'entire/676b805-e3b0c4'
```

Both sessions share the **same shadow branch** because no commit happened between them (same BaseCommit). This complicates the scenario.

Let me add a commit between the sessions so they have different shadow branches:

> TOOL

> AGENT

All tests pass. Let me summarize what we discovered:

## Summary: Bug Validation

### What catches the bug

**Unit tests catch the bug** because they directly manipulate the state to create the exact conditions:
```go
// In TestPostCommit_EndedSessionCarryForward_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit:
endedState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0  // Forces hasNew=true
```

This creates the scenario where:
- `hasNew = true` (transcript lines > CheckpointTranscriptStart)
- Files don't overlap
- Without fix: session is incorrectly condensed
- With fix: overlap check prevents condensation

### Why integration tests don't catch it directly

The integration tests go through the **full hook flow**, which properly manages `CheckpointTranscriptStart`:

1. `Stop` hook fires → `CheckpointTranscriptStart` updated to current transcript length
2. On subsequent commits → `hasNew = (transcript == CheckpointTranscriptStart) = false`
3. Since `hasNew = false`, the session isn't condensed **for the right reason** (no new content) rather than because of the overlap check

The bug manifests when `hasNew = true` but files don't overlap - which requires either:
- A bug that fails to update `CheckpointTranscriptStart`
- An agent crash mid-turn before checkpoint is finalized
- State corruption

### Value of the integration tests

The integration tests still provide value:
1. **End-to-end flow coverage** - verify hooks work together correctly
2. **Regression tests** - protect against future changes breaking the flow
3. **Documentation** - demonstrate expected behavior in realistic scenarios

The unit tests are the primary bug catchers, while integration tests provide flow validation.

  2. On subsequent commits → hasNew = (transcript == CheckpointTranscriptStart) = false
  3. Since hasNew = false, the session isn't condensed for the right reason (no new content) rather than because of the overlap check

Yes, a bit. Let me be honest about what I've found:

## What I verified

1. **`CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0` after carry-forward** (line 1883) - so `hasNew` WILL be true on subsequent commits

2. **Unit tests catch the bug** - I confirmed `TestPostCommit_EndedSessionCarryForward_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit` fails without the fix (it logs "Condensed session ended-ca" when it shouldn't)

3. **Integration tests pass both with and without the fix** - I can't figure out why

## What I don't understand

There's something in the integration test hook flow that behaves differently than the direct unit test code path. I've been going in circles trying to trace it.

## My correction

You're right - my earlier explanation was wrong:

```
❌ WRONG: Stop hook updates CheckpointTranscriptStart
✓ CORRECT: CheckpointTranscriptStart is updated during CONDENSATION, 
           and reset to 0 during CARRY-FORWARD
```

So after carry-forward, `hasNew` IS true, which is exactly why the fix is needed.

Do you want me to keep digging into why the integration tests don't catch it, or should we accept that the unit tests provide the bug coverage and the integration tests provide flow coverage?

   507→func (h *postCommitActionHandler) HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched(state *session.State) error {
   508→	shouldCondense := len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 && h.shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck(state.Phase.IsActive())
   509→
   510→	logging.Debug(h.logCtx, "post-commit: HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched decision",
   511→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   512→		slog.String("phase", string(state.Phase)),
   513→		slog.Bool("has_new", h.hasNew),
   514→		slog.Int("files_touched", len(state.FilesTouched)),
   515→		slog.Bool("should_condense", shouldCondense),
   516→		slog.String("shadow_branch", h.shadowBranchName),
   517→	)
   518→
   519→	if shouldCondense {
   520→		h.condensed = h.s.condenseAndUpdateState(h.logCtx, h.repo, h.checkpointID, state, h.head, h.shadowBranchName, h.shadowBranchesToDelete, h.committedFileSet)
   521→	} else {
   522→		h.s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(h.logCtx, state, h.newHead)
   523→	}
   524→	return nil
   525→}
   526→
   527→// shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck returns true if the session should be condensed
   528→// into this commit. Requires both that hasNew is true AND that the session's files
   529→// overlap with the committed files with matching content.
   530→//
   531→// This prevents stale sessions (ACTIVE sessions where the agent was killed, or
   532→// ENDED/IDLE sessions with carry-forward files) from being condensed into every
   533→// unrelated commit.
   534→//
   535→// filesTouchedBefore is populated from:
   536→//   - state.FilesTouched for IDLE/ENDED sessions (set via SaveStep/SaveTaskStep -> mergeFilesTouched)
   537→//   - transcript extraction for ACTIVE sessions when FilesTouched is empty
   538→//
   539→// When filesTouchedBefore is empty:
   540→//   - For ACTIVE sessions: fail-open (trust hasNew) because the agent may be
   541→//     mid-turn before any files are saved to state.
   542→//   - For IDLE/ENDED sessions: return false because there are no files to
   543→//     overlap with the commit.
   544→func (h *postCommitActionHandler) shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck(isActive bool) bool {
   545→	if !h.hasNew {
   546→		return false
   547→	}
   548→	if len(h.filesTouchedBefore) == 0 {
   549→		return isActive // ACTIVE: fail-open; IDLE/ENDED: no files = no overlap
   550→	}
   551→	// Only check files that were actually changed in this commit.
   552→	// Without this, files that exist in the tree but weren't changed
   553→	// would pass the "modified file" check in filesOverlapWithContent
   554→	// (because the file exists in the parent tree), causing stale
   555→	// sessions to be incorrectly condensed.
   556→	var committedTouchedFiles []string
   557→	for _, f := range h.filesTouchedBefore {
   558→		if _, ok := h.committedFileSet[f]; ok {
   559→			committedTouchedFiles = append(committedTouchedFiles, f)
   560→		}
   561→	}
   562→	if len(committedTouchedFiles) == 0 {
   563→		return false
   564→	}
   565→	return filesOverlapWithContent(h.repo, h.shadowBranchName, h.commit, committedTouchedFiles)
   566→}
   567→
   568→func (h *postCommitActionHandler) HandleDiscardIfNoFiles(state *session.State) error {
   569→	if len(state.FilesTouched) == 0 {
   570→		logging.Debug(h.logCtx, "post-commit: skipping empty ended session (no files to condense)",
   571→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   572→		)
   573→	}
   574→	h.s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(h.logCtx, state, h.newHead)
   575→	return nil
   576→}
   577→
   578→func (h *postCommitActionHandler) HandleWarnStaleSession(_ *session.State) error {
   579→	// Not produced by EventGitCommit; no-op for exhaustiveness.
   580→	return nil
   581→}
   582→
   583→// During rebase/cherry-pick/revert operations, phase transitions are skipped entirely.
   584→//
   585→//nolint:unparam // error return required by interface but hooks must return nil
   586→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PostCommit() error {
   587→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   588→
   589→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   590→	if err != nil {
   591→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   592→	}
   593→
   594→	// Get HEAD commit to check for trailer
   595→	head, err := repo.Head()
   596→	if err != nil {
   597→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   598→	}
   599→

     1→//go:build integration
     2→
     3→package integration
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"testing"
