     8→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session"
     9→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy"
    10→)
    11→
    12→// TestCarryForward_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit verifies that a session with
    13→// carry-forward files is NOT condensed into a commit that doesn't touch those files.
    14→//
    15→// This is a regression test for the bug where sessions with carry-forward files
    16→// would be re-condensed into every subsequent commit indefinitely. The root cause
    17→// was that HandleCondense and HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched only checked hasNew
    18→// (shadow branch has content) but didn't verify that the committed files actually
    19→// overlapped with the session's FilesTouched.
    20→//
    21→// Scenario:
    22→// 1. Session 1 creates file1.txt and file2.txt
    23→// 2. User commits ONLY file1.txt (partial commit)
    24→// 3. Session 1 gets carry-forward: FilesTouched = ["file2.txt"]
    25→// 4. Session 1 ends
    26→// 5. New session 2 creates file3.txt (unrelated to session 1)
    27→// 6. Session 2 commits file3.txt
    28→// 7. Verify: Session 1 was NOT condensed (FilesTouched preserved, StepCount unchanged)
    29→func TestCarryForward_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit(t *testing.T) {
    30→	t.Parallel()
    31→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
    32→	defer env.Cleanup()
    33→
    34→	// ========================================
    35→	// Setup
    36→	// ========================================
    37→	env.InitRepo()
    38→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
    39→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
    40→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
    41→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/carry-forward-test")
    42→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
    43→
    44→	// ========================================
    45→	// Phase 1: Session 1 creates multiple files
    46→	// ========================================
    47→	t.Log("Phase 1: Session 1 creates file1.txt and file2.txt")
    48→
    49→	session1 := env.NewSession()
    50→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session1.ID); err != nil {
    51→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit for session1 failed: %v", err)
    52→	}
    53→
    54→	// Create two files
    55→	env.WriteFile("file1.txt", "content from session 1 - file 1")
    56→	env.WriteFile("file2.txt", "content from session 1 - file 2")
    57→	session1.CreateTranscript(
    58→		"Create file1 and file2",
    59→		[]FileChange{
    60→			{Path: "file1.txt", Content: "content from session 1 - file 1"},
    61→			{Path: "file2.txt", Content: "content from session 1 - file 2"},
    62→		},
    63→	)
    64→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session1.ID, session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
    65→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop for session1 failed: %v", err)
    66→	}
    67→
    68→	// Verify session1 is IDLE with both files in FilesTouched
    69→	state1, err := env.GetSessionState(session1.ID)
    70→	if err != nil {
    71→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session1 failed: %v", err)
    72→	}
    73→	if state1.Phase != session.PhaseIdle {
    74→		t.Fatalf("Expected session1 to be IDLE, got %s", state1.Phase)
    75→	}
    76→	t.Logf("Session1 FilesTouched before partial commit: %v", state1.FilesTouched)
    77→
    78→	// ========================================
    79→	// Phase 2: Partial commit - only file1.txt
    80→	// ========================================
    81→	t.Log("Phase 2: Committing only file1.txt (partial commit)")
    82→
    83→	env.GitAdd("file1.txt")
    84→	// Note: file2.txt is NOT staged
    85→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Partial commit: only file1", "file1.txt")
    86→
    87→	// Verify carry-forward: file2.txt should remain in FilesTouched
    88→	state1, err = env.GetSessionState(session1.ID)
    89→	if err != nil {
    90→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session1 after partial commit failed: %v", err)
    91→	}
    92→	t.Logf("Session1 FilesTouched after partial commit: %v", state1.FilesTouched)
    93→
    94→	// file2.txt should be carried forward (not committed yet)
    95→	hasFile2 := false
    96→	for _, f := range state1.FilesTouched {
    97→		if f == "file2.txt" {
    98→			hasFile2 = true
    99→			break
   100→		}
   101→	}
   102→	if !hasFile2 {
   103→		t.Fatalf("Expected file2.txt to be carried forward in FilesTouched, got: %v", state1.FilesTouched)
   104→	}
   105→
   106→	// Record state for later comparison
   107→	session1StepCountAfterPartial := state1.StepCount
   108→	session1BaseCommitAfterPartial := state1.BaseCommit
   109→
   110→	// ========================================
   111→	// Phase 3: End session 1 (simulating user closes agent)
   112→	// ========================================
   113→	t.Log("Phase 3: Ending session 1")
   114→
   115→	state1.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
   116→	if err := env.WriteSessionState(session1.ID, state1); err != nil {
   117→		t.Fatalf("WriteSessionState for session1 failed: %v", err)
   118→	}
   119→
   120→	// ========================================
   121→	// Phase 4: New session 2 creates unrelated file
   122→	// ========================================
   123→	t.Log("Phase 4: Session 2 creates file3.txt (unrelated to session 1)")
   124→
   125→	session2 := env.NewSession()
   126→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session2.ID); err != nil {
   127→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit for session2 failed: %v", err)
   128→	}
   129→
   130→	env.WriteFile("file3.txt", "content from session 2")
   131→	session2.CreateTranscript(
   132→		"Create file3",
   133→		[]FileChange{{Path: "file3.txt", Content: "content from session 2"}},
   134→	)
   135→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session2.ID, session2.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   136→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop for session2 failed: %v", err)
   137→	}
   138→
   139→	// Set session2 to ACTIVE (simulating mid-turn commit)
   140→	state2, err := env.GetSessionState(session2.ID)
   141→	if err != nil {
   142→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session2 failed: %v", err)
   143→	}
   144→	state2.Phase = session.PhaseActive
   145→	if err := env.WriteSessionState(session2.ID, state2); err != nil {
   146→		t.Fatalf("WriteSessionState for session2 failed: %v", err)
   147→	}
   148→
   149→	// ========================================
   150→	// Phase 5: Commit file3.txt (unrelated to session 1's files)
   151→	// ========================================
   152→	t.Log("Phase 5: Committing file3.txt from session 2")
   153→
   154→	env.GitAdd("file3.txt")
   155→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add file3 from session 2", "file3.txt")
   156→
   157→	finalHead := env.GetHeadHash()
   158→	t.Logf("Final HEAD: %s", finalHead[:7])
   159→
   160→	// ========================================
   161→	// Phase 6: Verify session 1 was NOT condensed
   162→	// ========================================
   163→	t.Log("Phase 6: Verifying session 1 was NOT condensed into unrelated commit")
   164→
   165→	state1After, err := env.GetSessionState(session1.ID)
   166→	if err != nil {
   167→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session1 after session2 commit failed: %v", err)
   168→	}
   169→
   170→	// StepCount should be unchanged (condensation resets it)
   171→	if state1After.StepCount != session1StepCountAfterPartial {
   172→		t.Errorf("Session 1 StepCount changed! Expected %d, got %d (was incorrectly condensed)",
   173→			session1StepCountAfterPartial, state1After.StepCount)
   174→	}
   175→
   176→	// BaseCommit should be unchanged (ENDED sessions don't get BaseCommit updated)
   177→	if state1After.BaseCommit != session1BaseCommitAfterPartial {
   178→		t.Errorf("Session 1 BaseCommit changed! Expected %s, got %s",
   179→			session1BaseCommitAfterPartial[:7], state1After.BaseCommit[:7])
   180→	}
   181→
   182→	// FilesTouched should still have file2.txt (not cleared by condensation)
   183→	hasFile2After := false
   184→	for _, f := range state1After.FilesTouched {
   185→		if f == "file2.txt" {
   186→			hasFile2After = true
   187→			break
   188→		}
   189→	}
   190→	if !hasFile2After {
   191→		t.Errorf("Session 1 FilesTouched was cleared! Expected file2.txt to remain, got: %v",
   192→			state1After.FilesTouched)
   193→	}
   194→
   195→	// Phase should still be ENDED
   196→	if state1After.Phase != session.PhaseEnded {
   197→		t.Errorf("Session 1 phase changed! Expected ENDED, got %s", state1After.Phase)
   198→	}
   199→
   200→	// ========================================
   201→	// Phase 7: Verify session 2 WAS condensed
   202→	// ========================================
   203→	t.Log("Phase 7: Verifying session 2 WAS condensed")
   204→
   205→	state2After, err := env.GetSessionState(session2.ID)
   206→	if err != nil {
   207→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session2 after commit failed: %v", err)
   208→	}
   209→
   210→	// Session 2's BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD
   211→	if state2After.BaseCommit != finalHead {
   212→		t.Errorf("Session 2 BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD. Expected %s, got %s",
   213→			finalHead[:7], state2After.BaseCommit[:7])
   214→	}
   215→
   216→	t.Log("Test completed successfully")
   217→}
   218→
   219→// TestCarryForward_NotCondensedIntoMultipleUnrelatedCommits verifies that a session
   220→// with carry-forward files is NOT condensed into MULTIPLE subsequent unrelated commits.
   221→//
   222→// This is a regression test for the "repeat on every commit" bug where sessions
   223→// with carry-forward files would be re-condensed into every subsequent commit
   224→// indefinitely, polluting checkpoints.
   225→//
   226→// Scenario:
   227→// 1. Session 1 creates file1.txt and file2.txt
   228→// 2. User commits only file1.txt (partial commit)
   229→// 3. Session 1 gets carry-forward: FilesTouched = ["file2.txt"]
   230→// 4. Make 3 unrelated commits (file3.txt, file4.txt, file5.txt)
   231→// 5. Verify: Session 1 was NOT condensed into ANY of those commits
   232→// 6. Finally commit file2.txt
   233→// 7. Verify: Session 1 IS condensed (carry-forward consumed)
   234→func TestCarryForward_NotCondensedIntoMultipleUnrelatedCommits(t *testing.T) {
   235→	t.Parallel()
   236→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
   237→	defer env.Cleanup()
   238→
   239→	// ========================================
   240→	// Setup
   241→	// ========================================
   242→	env.InitRepo()
   243→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
   244→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
   245→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
   246→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/repeat-condensation-test")
   247→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   248→
   249→	// ========================================
   250→	// Phase 1: Session 1 creates multiple files
   251→	// ========================================
   252→	t.Log("Phase 1: Session 1 creates file1.txt and file2.txt")
   253→
   254→	session1 := env.NewSession()
   255→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session1.ID); err != nil {
   256→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit for session1 failed: %v", err)
   257→	}
   258→
   259→	env.WriteFile("file1.txt", "content from session 1 - file 1")
   260→	env.WriteFile("file2.txt", "content from session 1 - file 2")
   261→	session1.CreateTranscript(
   262→		"Create file1 and file2",
   263→		[]FileChange{
   264→			{Path: "file1.txt", Content: "content from session 1 - file 1"},
   265→			{Path: "file2.txt", Content: "content from session 1 - file 2"},
   266→		},
   267→	)
   268→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session1.ID, session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   269→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop for session1 failed: %v", err)
   270→	}
   271→
   272→	// ========================================
   273→	// Phase 2: Partial commit - only file1.txt
   274→	// ========================================
   275→	t.Log("Phase 2: Committing only file1.txt (partial commit)")
   276→
   277→	env.GitAdd("file1.txt")
   278→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Partial commit: only file1", "file1.txt")
   279→
   280→	// Verify carry-forward
   281→	state1, err := env.GetSessionState(session1.ID)
   282→	if err != nil {
   283→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session1 after partial commit failed: %v", err)
   284→	}
   285→	t.Logf("Session1 FilesTouched after partial commit: %v", state1.FilesTouched)
   286→
   287→	// End session 1 and force hasNew=true by setting CheckpointTranscriptStart=0
   288→	// This simulates the bug scenario where sessionHasNewContent returns true
   289→	// because the transcript has "grown" since the (reset) checkpoint start.
   290→	state1.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
   291→	state1.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0 // Force hasNew=true for subsequent commits
   292→	if err := env.WriteSessionState(session1.ID, state1); err != nil {
   293→		t.Fatalf("WriteSessionState for session1 failed: %v", err)
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	// Record initial state for comparison
   297→	session1InitialStepCount := state1.StepCount
   298→
   299→	// ========================================
   300→	// Phase 3: Make multiple unrelated commits
   301→	// ========================================
   302→	unrelatedFiles := []string{"file3.txt", "file4.txt", "file5.txt"}
   303→
   304→	for i, fileName := range unrelatedFiles {
   305→		t.Logf("Phase 3.%d: Making unrelated commit with %s", i+1, fileName)
   306→
   307→		env.WriteFile(fileName, "unrelated content "+fileName)
   308→		env.GitAdd(fileName)
   309→		env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add "+fileName, fileName)
   310→
   311→		// Verify session 1 was NOT condensed after each commit
   312→		state1, err = env.GetSessionState(session1.ID)
   313→		if err != nil {
   314→			t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session1 after commit %d failed: %v", i+1, err)
   315→		}
   316→
   317→		if state1.StepCount != session1InitialStepCount {
   318→			t.Errorf("After commit %d (%s): Session 1 StepCount changed from %d to %d (incorrectly condensed!)",
   319→				i+1, fileName, session1InitialStepCount, state1.StepCount)
   320→		}
   321→
   322→		// FilesTouched should still have file2.txt
   323→		hasFile2 := false
   324→		for _, f := range state1.FilesTouched {
   325→			if f == "file2.txt" {
   326→				hasFile2 = true
   327→				break
   328→			}
   329→		}
   330→		if !hasFile2 {
   331→			t.Errorf("After commit %d (%s): Session 1 FilesTouched was cleared! Expected file2.txt, got: %v",
   332→				i+1, fileName, state1.FilesTouched)
   333→		}
   334→
   335→		t.Logf("  -> Session 1 correctly NOT condensed (StepCount=%d, FilesTouched=%v)",
   336→			state1.StepCount, state1.FilesTouched)
   337→	}
   338→
   339→	// ========================================
   340→	// Phase 4: Finally commit file2.txt (the carry-forward file)
   341→	// ========================================
   342→	t.Log("Phase 4: Committing file2.txt (the carry-forward file)")
   343→
   344→	env.GitAdd("file2.txt")
   345→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add file2 (carry-forward)", "file2.txt")
   346→
   347→	// ========================================
   348→	// Phase 5: Verify session 1 WAS condensed this time
   349→	// ========================================
   350→	t.Log("Phase 5: Verifying session 1 WAS condensed when its file was committed")
   351→
   352→	state1, err = env.GetSessionState(session1.ID)
   353→	if err != nil {
   354→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session1 after file2 commit failed: %v", err)
   355→	}
   356→
   357→	// StepCount should be reset (condensation happened)
   358→	if state1.StepCount != 0 {
   359→		t.Errorf("Session 1 StepCount should be 0 after condensation, got %d", state1.StepCount)
   360→	}
   361→
   362→	// FilesTouched should be empty (carry-forward consumed)
   363→	if len(state1.FilesTouched) != 0 {
   364→		t.Errorf("Session 1 FilesTouched should be empty after condensation, got: %v", state1.FilesTouched)
   365→	}
   366→
   367→	t.Log("Test completed successfully - session correctly condensed only when its file was committed")
   368→}
   369→
   370→// TestCarryForward_NewSessionCommitDoesNotCondenseOldSession verifies that when
   371→// an old session has carry-forward files and a NEW session commits unrelated files,
   372→// the old session is NOT condensed into the new session's commit.
   373→//
   374→// This tests the interaction between multiple sessions where:
   375→// 1. Old session has carry-forward files (file2.txt)
   376→// 2. New session creates and commits different files (file6.txt)
   377→// 3. Old session should remain untouched
   378→func TestCarryForward_NewSessionCommitDoesNotCondenseOldSession(t *testing.T) {
   379→	t.Parallel()
   380→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
   381→	defer env.Cleanup()
   382→
   383→	// ========================================
   384→	// Setup
   385→	// ========================================
   386→	env.InitRepo()
   387→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
   388→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
   389→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
   390→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/multi-session-carry-forward")
   391→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   392→
   393→	// ========================================
   394→	// Phase 1: Session 1 creates files, partial commit, ends with carry-forward
   395→	// ========================================
   396→	t.Log("Phase 1: Session 1 creates file1.txt and file2.txt")
   397→
   398→	session1 := env.NewSession()
   399→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session1.ID); err != nil {
   400→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit for session1 failed: %v", err)
   401→	}
   402→
   403→	env.WriteFile("file1.txt", "content from session 1 - file 1")
   404→	env.WriteFile("file2.txt", "content from session 1 - file 2")
   405→	session1.CreateTranscript(
   406→		"Create file1 and file2",
   407→		[]FileChange{
   408→			{Path: "file1.txt", Content: "content from session 1 - file 1"},
   409→			{Path: "file2.txt", Content: "content from session 1 - file 2"},
   410→		},
   411→	)
   412→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session1.ID, session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   413→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop for session1 failed: %v", err)
   414→	}
   415→
   416→	// Partial commit - only file1.txt
   417→	t.Log("Phase 1b: Partial commit - only file1.txt")
   418→	env.GitAdd("file1.txt")
   419→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Partial commit: only file1", "file1.txt")
   420→
   421→	// End session 1
   422→	state1, err := env.GetSessionState(session1.ID)
   423→	if err != nil {
   424→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session1 failed: %v", err)
   425→	}
   426→	state1.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
   427→	if err := env.WriteSessionState(session1.ID, state1); err != nil {
   428→		t.Fatalf("WriteSessionState for session1 failed: %v", err)
   429→	}
   430→
   431→	// Verify carry-forward
   432→	state1, err = env.GetSessionState(session1.ID)
   433→	if err != nil {
   434→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session1 failed: %v", err)
   435→	}
   436→	t.Logf("Session1 (ENDED) FilesTouched: %v", state1.FilesTouched)
   437→
   438→	session1StepCount := state1.StepCount
   439→
   440→	// ========================================
   441→	// Phase 2: Make some unrelated commits (simulating time passing)
   442→	// ========================================
   443→	t.Log("Phase 2: Making unrelated commits")
   444→
   445→	for _, fileName := range []string{"file3.txt", "file4.txt"} {
   446→		env.WriteFile(fileName, "unrelated content")
   447→		env.GitAdd(fileName)
   448→		env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add "+fileName, fileName)
   449→	}
   450→
   451→	// ========================================
   452→	// Phase 3: NEW session 2 starts and creates file6.txt
   453→	// ========================================
   454→	t.Log("Phase 3: Session 2 starts and creates file6.txt")
   455→
   456→	session2 := env.NewSession()
   457→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session2.ID); err != nil {
   458→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit for session2 failed: %v", err)
   459→	}
   460→
   461→	env.WriteFile("file6.txt", "content from session 2")
   462→	session2.CreateTranscript(
   463→		"Create file6",
   464→		[]FileChange{{Path: "file6.txt", Content: "content from session 2"}},
   465→	)
   466→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session2.ID, session2.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   467→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop for session2 failed: %v", err)
   468→	}
   469→
   470→	// Set session2 to ACTIVE
   471→	state2, err := env.GetSessionState(session2.ID)
   472→	if err != nil {
   473→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session2 failed: %v", err)
   474→	}
   475→	state2.Phase = session.PhaseActive
   476→	if err := env.WriteSessionState(session2.ID, state2); err != nil {
   477→		t.Fatalf("WriteSessionState for session2 failed: %v", err)
   478→	}
   479→
   480→	// ========================================
   481→	// Phase 4: Commit file6.txt (session 2's file)
   482→	// ========================================
   483→	t.Log("Phase 4: Committing file6.txt from session 2")
   484→
   485→	env.GitAdd("file6.txt")
   486→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add file6 from session 2", "file6.txt")
   487→
   488→	finalHead := env.GetHeadHash()
   489→
   490→	// ========================================
   491→	// Phase 5: Verify session 1 was NOT condensed
   492→	// ========================================
   493→	t.Log("Phase 5: Verifying session 1 (with carry-forward) was NOT condensed")
   494→
   495→	state1After, err := env.GetSessionState(session1.ID)
   496→	if err != nil {
   497→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session1 after session2 commit failed: %v", err)
   498→	}
   499→
   500→	// StepCount should be unchanged
   501→	if state1After.StepCount != session1StepCount {
   502→		t.Errorf("Session 1 StepCount changed! Expected %d, got %d (incorrectly condensed into session 2's commit)",
   503→			session1StepCount, state1After.StepCount)
   504→	}
   505→
   506→	// FilesTouched should still have file2.txt
   507→	hasFile2 := false
   508→	for _, f := range state1After.FilesTouched {
   509→		if f == "file2.txt" {
   510→			hasFile2 = true
   511→			break
   512→		}
   513→	}
   514→	if !hasFile2 {
   515→		t.Errorf("Session 1 FilesTouched was cleared! Expected file2.txt, got: %v", state1After.FilesTouched)
   516→	}
   517→
   518→	t.Logf("Session 1 correctly preserved: StepCount=%d, FilesTouched=%v", state1After.StepCount, state1After.FilesTouched)
   519→
   520→	// ========================================
   521→	// Phase 6: Verify session 2 WAS condensed
   522→	// ========================================
   523→	t.Log("Phase 6: Verifying session 2 WAS condensed")
   524→
   525→	state2After, err := env.GetSessionState(session2.ID)
   526→	if err != nil {
   527→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session2 after commit failed: %v", err)
   528→	}
   529→
   530→	if state2After.BaseCommit != finalHead {
   531→		t.Errorf("Session 2 BaseCommit should be updated. Expected %s, got %s",
   532→			finalHead[:7], state2After.BaseCommit[:7])
   533→	}
   534→
   535→	// ========================================
   536→	// Phase 7: Finally commit file2.txt (session 1's carry-forward file)
   537→	// ========================================
   538→	t.Log("Phase 7: Committing file2.txt (session 1's carry-forward file)")
   539→
   540→	env.GitAdd("file2.txt")
   541→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add file2 (session 1 carry-forward)", "file2.txt")
   542→
   543→	// ========================================
   544→	// Phase 8: Verify session 1 WAS condensed this time
   545→	// ========================================
   546→	t.Log("Phase 8: Verifying session 1 WAS condensed when its carry-forward file was committed")
   547→
   548→	state1Final, err := env.GetSessionState(session1.ID)
   549→	if err != nil {
   550→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session1 after file2 commit failed: %v", err)
   551→	}
   552→
   553→	// StepCount should be reset to 0 (condensation happened)
   554→	if state1Final.StepCount != 0 {
   555→		t.Errorf("Session 1 StepCount should be 0 after condensation, got %d", state1Final.StepCount)
   556→	}
   557→
   558→	// FilesTouched should be empty (carry-forward consumed)
   559→	if len(state1Final.FilesTouched) != 0 {
   560→		t.Errorf("Session 1 FilesTouched should be empty after condensation, got: %v", state1Final.FilesTouched)
   561→	}
   562→
   563→	t.Log("Test completed successfully:")
   564→	t.Log("  - Session 1 NOT condensed into session 2's commit (file6.txt)")
   565→	t.Log("  - Session 1 WAS condensed when its own file (file2.txt) was committed")
   566→}
   567→
   568→// TestStaleActiveSession_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit verifies that a stale
   569→// ACTIVE session (agent resumed then killed) is NOT condensed into an unrelated
   570→// commit from a different session.
   571→//
   572→// This is a regression test for the bug where ACTIVE sessions would always be
   573→// condensed (hasNew=true) without checking file overlap.
   574→//
   575→// Scenario:
   576→// 1. Session 1 creates file1.txt, Stop hook fires (checkpoint saved)
   577→// 2. Session 1 is resumed (back to ACTIVE), does more work
   578→// 3. Agent is killed (stays ACTIVE, new work not committed)
   579→// 4. New session 2 creates file2.txt (unrelated)
   580→// 5. Session 2 commits file2.txt
   581→// 6. Verify: Session 1 was NOT condensed (its files weren't in the commit)
   582→func TestStaleActiveSession_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit(t *testing.T) {
   583→	t.Parallel()
   584→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
   585→	defer env.Cleanup()
   586→
   587→	// ========================================
   588→	// Setup
   589→	// ========================================
   590→	env.InitRepo()
   591→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
   592→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
   593→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
   594→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/stale-active-test")
   595→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   596→
   597→	// ========================================
   598→	// Phase 1: Session 1 creates file, Stop hook fires (creates checkpoint)
   599→	// ========================================
   600→	t.Log("Phase 1: Session 1 creates file1.txt, Stop hook fires")
   601→
   602→	session1 := env.NewSession()
   603→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session1.ID); err != nil {
   604→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit for session1 failed: %v", err)
   605→	}
   606→
   607→	env.WriteFile("file1.txt", "content from session 1")
   608→	session1.CreateTranscript(
   609→		"Create file1",
   610→		[]FileChange{{Path: "file1.txt", Content: "content from session 1"}},
   611→	)
   612→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session1.ID, session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   613→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop for session1 failed: %v", err)
   614→	}
   615→
   616→	// Session 1 is now IDLE with a checkpoint
   617→	state1, err := env.GetSessionState(session1.ID)
   618→	if err != nil {
   619→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session1 failed: %v", err)
   620→	}
   621→	t.Logf("Session1 after Stop: Phase=%s, StepCount=%d, CheckpointTranscriptStart=%d",
   622→		state1.Phase, state1.StepCount, state1.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   623→
   624→	// ========================================
   625→	// Phase 2: Simulate agent resume + crash (ACTIVE with hasNew=true)
   626→	// Set ACTIVE and reset CheckpointTranscriptStart to simulate:
   627→	// - Agent was resumed (ACTIVE)
   628→	// - Did more work (transcript grew, but not saved to checkpoint yet)
   629→	// - Then crashed (stuck ACTIVE with hasNew=true)
   630→	// ========================================
   631→	t.Log("Phase 2: Simulating agent resume then crash (ACTIVE with hasNew=true)")
   632→
   633→	state1.Phase = session.PhaseActive
   634→	state1.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0 // Force hasNew=true (transcript > 0)
   635→	if err := env.WriteSessionState(session1.ID, state1); err != nil {
   636→		t.Fatalf("WriteSessionState for session1 failed: %v", err)
   637→	}
   638→
   639→	// Record state for comparison
   640→	session1StepCount := state1.StepCount
   641→	t.Logf("Session1 (stale ACTIVE) StepCount: %d, CheckpointTranscriptStart: %d (forced to 0 for hasNew=true)",
   642→		session1StepCount, state1.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   643→
   644→	// ========================================
   645→	// Phase 2b: Make an unrelated commit to move HEAD forward
   646→	// This ensures Session 1 and Session 2 have DIFFERENT shadow branches
   647→	// (different BaseCommit), isolating their condensation behavior.
   648→	// ========================================
   649→	t.Log("Phase 2b: Making unrelated commit to separate shadow branches")
   650→	env.WriteFile("unrelated.txt", "unrelated content")
   651→	env.GitAdd("unrelated.txt")
   652→	env.GitCommit("Unrelated commit to move HEAD")
   653→
   654→	// ========================================
   655→	// Phase 3: New session 2 creates unrelated file
   656→	// ========================================
   657→	t.Log("Phase 3: Session 2 creates file2.txt (unrelated to session 1)")
   658→
   659→	session2 := env.NewSession()
   660→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session2.ID); err != nil {
   661→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit for session2 failed: %v", err)
   662→	}
   663→
   664→	env.WriteFile("file2.txt", "content from session 2")
   665→	session2.CreateTranscript(
   666→		"Create file2",
   667→		[]FileChange{{Path: "file2.txt", Content: "content from session 2"}},
   668→	)
   669→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session2.ID, session2.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   670→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop for session2 failed: %v", err)
   671→	}
   672→
   673→	// Set session2 to ACTIVE
   674→	state2, err := env.GetSessionState(session2.ID)
   675→	if err != nil {
   676→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session2 failed: %v", err)
   677→	}
   678→	state2.Phase = session.PhaseActive
   679→	if err := env.WriteSessionState(session2.ID, state2); err != nil {
   680→		t.Fatalf("WriteSessionState for session2 failed: %v", err)
   681→	}
   682→
   683→	// ========================================
   684→	// Phase 4: Commit file2.txt (unrelated to session 1)
   685→	// ========================================
   686→	t.Log("Phase 4: Committing file2.txt from session 2")
   687→
   688→	env.GitAdd("file2.txt")
   689→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add file2 from session 2", "file2.txt")
   690→
   691→	finalHead := env.GetHeadHash()
   692→	t.Logf("Final HEAD: %s", finalHead[:7])
   693→
   694→	// ========================================
   695→	// Phase 5: Verify stale session 1 was NOT condensed
   696→	// ========================================
   697→	t.Log("Phase 5: Verifying stale session 1 was NOT condensed")
   698→
   699→	state1After, err := env.GetSessionState(session1.ID)
   700→	if err != nil {
   701→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session1 after session2 commit failed: %v", err)
   702→	}
   703→
   704→	// StepCount should be unchanged (condensation resets it)
   705→	if state1After.StepCount != session1StepCount {
   706→		t.Errorf("Stale session 1 StepCount changed! Expected %d, got %d (was incorrectly condensed)",
   707→			session1StepCount, state1After.StepCount)
   708→	}
   709→
   710→	// Note: BaseCommit WILL be updated for ACTIVE sessions (even stale ones) because
   711→	// updateBaseCommitIfChanged only skips IDLE/ENDED sessions. This is fine - the
   712→	// key fix is the overlap check that prevents condensation, not BaseCommit updates.
   713→	// A stale ACTIVE session that gets resumed later needs to know the current HEAD.
   714→
   715→	// FilesTouched should still have file1.txt (not cleared by condensation)
   716→	hasFile1 := false
   717→	for _, f := range state1After.FilesTouched {
   718→		if f == "file1.txt" {
   719→			hasFile1 = true
   720→			break
   721→		}
   722→	}
   723→	if !hasFile1 {
   724→		t.Errorf("Stale session 1 FilesTouched was cleared! Expected file1.txt, got: %v",
   725→			state1After.FilesTouched)
   726→	}
   727→
   728→	// ========================================
   729→	// Phase 6: Verify session 2 WAS condensed
   730→	// ========================================
   731→	t.Log("Phase 6: Verifying session 2 WAS condensed")
   732→
   733→	state2After, err := env.GetSessionState(session2.ID)
   734→	if err != nil {
   735→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session2 after commit failed: %v", err)
   736→	}
   737→
   738→	if state2After.BaseCommit != finalHead {
   739→		t.Errorf("Session 2 BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD. Expected %s, got %s",
   740→			finalHead[:7], state2After.BaseCommit[:7])
   741→	}
   742→
   743→	t.Log("Test completed successfully")
   744→}
   745→
   746→// TestIdleSessionEmptyFilesTouched_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit verifies that
   747→// an IDLE session with empty FilesTouched (e.g., conversation-only session) is NOT
   748→// condensed into an unrelated commit.
   749→//
   750→// This is a regression test for the fail-open bug where shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck
   751→// would return true when filesTouchedBefore was empty, causing conversation-only
   752→// sessions to be condensed into every commit.
   753→//
   754→// Scenario:
   755→// 1. Session 1 has a conversation but doesn't modify any files
   756→// 2. New session 2 creates file1.txt
   757→// 3. Session 2 commits file1.txt
   758→// 4. Verify: Session 1 was NOT condensed
   759→func TestIdleSessionEmptyFilesTouched_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit(t *testing.T) {
   760→	t.Parallel()
   761→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
   762→	defer env.Cleanup()
   763→
   764→	// ========================================
   765→	// Setup
   766→	// ========================================
   767→	env.InitRepo()
   768→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
   769→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
   770→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
   771→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/empty-files-test")
   772→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   773→
   774→	// ========================================
   775→	// Phase 1: Session 1 - conversation only, no file changes
   776→	// ========================================
   777→	t.Log("Phase 1: Session 1 has conversation but no file changes")
   778→
   779→	session1 := env.NewSession()
   780→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session1.ID); err != nil {
   781→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit for session1 failed: %v", err)
   782→	}
   783→
   784→	// Create transcript with NO file changes
   785→	session1.CreateTranscript(
   786→		"What is the meaning of life?",
   787→		[]FileChange{}, // No files touched
   788→	)
   789→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session1.ID, session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   790→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop for session1 failed: %v", err)
   791→	}
   792→
   793→	// Verify session1 is IDLE with empty FilesTouched
   794→	state1, err := env.GetSessionState(session1.ID)
   795→	if err != nil {
   796→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session1 failed: %v", err)
   797→	}
   798→	if state1.Phase != session.PhaseIdle {
   799→		t.Fatalf("Expected session1 to be IDLE, got %s", state1.Phase)
   800→	}
   801→	if len(state1.FilesTouched) != 0 {
   802→		t.Fatalf("Expected session1 FilesTouched to be empty, got: %v", state1.FilesTouched)
   803→	}
   804→
   805→	session1StepCount := state1.StepCount
   806→	session1BaseCommit := state1.BaseCommit
   807→	t.Logf("Session1 (conversation-only) BaseCommit: %s, StepCount: %d",
   808→		session1BaseCommit[:7], session1StepCount)
   809→
   810→	// ========================================
   811→	// Phase 2: Session 2 creates a file
   812→	// ========================================
   813→	t.Log("Phase 2: Session 2 creates file1.txt")
   814→
   815→	session2 := env.NewSession()
   816→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session2.ID); err != nil {
   817→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit for session2 failed: %v", err)
   818→	}
   819→
   820→	env.WriteFile("file1.txt", "content from session 2")
   821→	session2.CreateTranscript(
   822→		"Create file1",
   823→		[]FileChange{{Path: "file1.txt", Content: "content from session 2"}},
   824→	)
   825→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session2.ID, session2.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   826→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop for session2 failed: %v", err)
   827→	}
   828→
   829→	// Set session2 to ACTIVE
   830→	state2, err := env.GetSessionState(session2.ID)
   831→	if err != nil {
   832→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session2 failed: %v", err)
   833→	}
   834→	state2.Phase = session.PhaseActive
   835→	if err := env.WriteSessionState(session2.ID, state2); err != nil {
   836→		t.Fatalf("WriteSessionState for session2 failed: %v", err)
   837→	}
   838→
   839→	// ========================================
   840→	// Phase 3: Commit file1.txt
   841→	// ========================================
   842→	t.Log("Phase 3: Committing file1.txt from session 2")
   843→
   844→	env.GitAdd("file1.txt")
   845→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add file1 from session 2", "file1.txt")
   846→
   847→	finalHead := env.GetHeadHash()
   848→
   849→	// ========================================
   850→	// Phase 4: Verify conversation-only session 1 was NOT condensed
   851→	// ========================================
   852→	t.Log("Phase 4: Verifying conversation-only session 1 was NOT condensed")
   853→
   854→	state1After, err := env.GetSessionState(session1.ID)
   855→	if err != nil {
   856→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session1 after session2 commit failed: %v", err)
   857→	}
   858→
   859→	// StepCount should be unchanged
   860→	if state1After.StepCount != session1StepCount {
   861→		t.Errorf("Conversation-only session 1 StepCount changed! Expected %d, got %d",
   862→			session1StepCount, state1After.StepCount)
   863→	}
   864→
   865→	// BaseCommit should be unchanged
   866→	if state1After.BaseCommit != session1BaseCommit {
   867→		t.Errorf("Conversation-only session 1 BaseCommit changed! Expected %s, got %s",
   868→			session1BaseCommit[:7], state1After.BaseCommit[:7])
   869→	}
   870→
   871→	// FilesTouched should still be empty
   872→	if len(state1After.FilesTouched) != 0 {
   873→		t.Errorf("Conversation-only session 1 FilesTouched should remain empty, got: %v",
   874→			state1After.FilesTouched)
   875→	}
   876→
   877→	// ========================================
   878→	// Phase 6: Verify session 2 WAS condensed
   879→	// ========================================
   880→	t.Log("Phase 6: Verifying session 2 WAS condensed")
   881→
   882→	state2After, err := env.GetSessionState(session2.ID)
   883→	if err != nil {
   884→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState for session2 after commit failed: %v", err)
   885→	}
   886→
   887→	if state2After.BaseCommit != finalHead {
   888→		t.Errorf("Session 2 BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD. Expected %s, got %s",
   889→			finalHead[:7], state2After.BaseCommit[:7])
   890→	}
   891→
   892→	t.Log("Test completed successfully")
   893→}
   894→

   623→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   624→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   625→		)
   626→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: hooks must be silent on failure
   627→	}
   628→
   629→	// Build transition context
   630→	isRebase := isGitSequenceOperation()
   631→	transitionCtx := session.TransitionContext{
   632→		IsRebaseInProgress: isRebase,
   633→	}
   634→
   635→	if isRebase {
   636→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: rebase/sequence in progress, skipping phase transitions",
   637→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   638→		)
   639→	}
   640→
   641→	// Track shadow branch names and whether they can be deleted
   642→	shadowBranchesToDelete := make(map[string]struct{})
   643→	// Track active sessions that were NOT condensed — their shadow branches must be preserved
   644→	uncondensedActiveOnBranch := make(map[string]bool)
   645→
   646→	newHead := head.Hash().String()
   647→	committedFileSet := filesChangedInCommit(commit)
   648→
   649→	for _, state := range sessions {
   650→		shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   651→
   652→		// Check for new content (needed for TransitionContext and condensation).
   653→		// Fail-open: if content check errors, assume new content exists so we
   654→		// don't silently skip data that should have been condensed.
   655→		//
   656→		// For ACTIVE sessions: the commit has a checkpoint trailer (verified above),
   657→		// meaning PrepareCommitMsg already determined this commit is session-related.
   658→		// The trailer is only added when either:
   659→		//   - No TTY (agent/subagent committing) — added unconditionally
   660→		//   - TTY (human committing) — added after content detection confirmed agent work
   661→		// In both cases, PrepareCommitMsg already validated this commit. We trust
   662→		// that decision here. Transcript-based re-validation is unreliable because
   663→		// subagent transcripts may not be available yet (subagent still running).
   664→		var hasNew bool
   665→		if state.Phase.IsActive() {
   666→			hasNew = true
   667→		} else {
   668→			var contentErr error
   669→			hasNew, contentErr = s.sessionHasNewContent(repo, state)
   670→			if contentErr != nil {
   671→				hasNew = true
   672→				logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: error checking session content, assuming new content",

   675→				)
   676→			}
   677→		}
   678→		transitionCtx.HasFilesTouched = len(state.FilesTouched) > 0
   679→
   680→		// Save FilesTouched BEFORE TransitionAndLog — the handler's condensation
   681→		// clears it, but we need the original list for carry-forward computation.
   682→		// For mid-session commits (ACTIVE, no shadow branch), state.FilesTouched may be empty
   683→		// because no SaveStep/Stop has been called yet. Extract files from transcript.
   684→		filesTouchedBefore := make([]string, len(state.FilesTouched))
   685→		copy(filesTouchedBefore, state.FilesTouched)
   686→		if len(filesTouchedBefore) == 0 && state.Phase.IsActive() && state.TranscriptPath != "" {
   687→			filesTouchedBefore = s.extractFilesFromLiveTranscript(state)
   688→		}

   692→			slog.Bool("is_active", state.Phase.IsActive()),
   693→			slog.String("transcript_path", state.TranscriptPath),
   694→			slog.Int("files_touched_before", len(filesTouchedBefore)),
   695→			slog.Any("files", filesTouchedBefore),

   698→		// Run the state machine transition with handler for strategy-specific actions.
   699→		handler := &postCommitActionHandler{

   702→			repo:                   repo,
   703→			checkpointID:           checkpointID,

   715→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: post-commit action handler error: %v\n", err)
   716→		}
   717→
   718→		// Record checkpoint ID for ACTIVE sessions so HandleTurnEnd can finalize
   719→		// with full transcript. IDLE/ENDED sessions already have complete transcripts.
   720→		// NOTE: This check runs AFTER TransitionAndLog updated the phase. It relies on
   721→		// ACTIVE + GitCommit → ACTIVE (phase stays ACTIVE). If that state machine
   722→		// transition ever changed, this guard would silently stop recording IDs.
   723→		if handler.condensed && state.Phase.IsActive() {

   727→		// Carry forward remaining uncommitted files so the next commit gets its
   728→		// own checkpoint ID. This applies to ALL phases — if a user splits their
   729→		// commit across two `git commit` invocations, each gets a 1:1 checkpoint.
   730→		// Uses content-aware comparison: if user did `git add -p` and committed
   731→		// partial changes, the file still has remaining agent changes to carry forward.
   732→		if handler.condensed {

   783→// condenseAndUpdateState runs condensation for a session and updates state afterward.
   784→// Returns true if condensation succeeded.
   785→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) condenseAndUpdateState(
   786→	logCtx context.Context,
   787→	repo *git.Repository,
   788→	checkpointID id.CheckpointID,
   789→	state *SessionState,
   790→	head *plumbing.Reference,
   791→	shadowBranchName string,
   792→	shadowBranchesToDelete map[string]struct{},
   793→	committedFiles map[string]struct{},
   794→) bool {

   796→	if err != nil {

> AGENT

All tests pass and the code is clean. Here's a summary of the verification:

## Summary

I successfully verified that the integration tests in `carry_forward_overlap_test.go` correctly catch the bug that this branch fixes.

### What was verified:

1. **With the fix**: All 7 integration tests pass:
   - `TestCarryForward_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit`
   - `TestCarryForward_NotCondensedIntoMultipleUnrelatedCommits`
   - `TestCarryForward_NewSessionCommitDoesNotCondenseOldSession`
   - `TestStaleActiveSession_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit`
   - `TestIdleSessionEmptyFilesTouched_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit`
   - `TestOldIdleSession_BaseCommitNotUpdated`
   - `TestOldEndedSession_BaseCommitNotUpdated`

2. **Without the fix** (temporarily reverted): The test fails with:
   ```
   Session 1 BaseCommit changed! Expected 85bef02, got 0602129
   ```

### The fix mechanism:

The key fix is in `shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck()` which:
1. Returns `false` if `hasNew` is false (no new content)
2. For IDLE/ENDED sessions with empty `filesTouchedBefore`: returns `false` (no files = no overlap possible)
3. Computes the intersection of `filesTouchedBefore` and `committedFileSet`
4. If no overlap, returns `false` (prevents condensation into unrelated commits)
5. If overlap exists, calls `filesOverlapWithContent()` to verify actual content matches

This prevents the bug where sessions with carry-forward files (or stale ACTIVE sessions) were being condensed into every subsequent commit indefinitely, polluting checkpoints with duplicate data.

    70→		return len(filesTouched) > 0

    72→
    73→	shadowTree, err := shadowCommit.Tree()
    74→	if err != nil {
    75→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "filesOverlapWithContent: failed to get shadow tree, falling back to filename check",
    76→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
    77→		)
    78→		return len(filesTouched) > 0
    79→	}
    80→
    81→	// Get the parent commit tree to determine if files are modified vs newly created.
    82→	// For modified files (exist in parent), we count as overlap regardless of content
    83→	// because the user is editing the session's work.
    84→	// For newly created files (don't exist in parent), we check content to detect
    85→	// the "reverted and replaced" scenario where user deleted session's work and
    86→	// created something completely different.
    87→	var parentTree *object.Tree
    88→	if headCommit.NumParents() > 0 {
    89→		if parent, err := headCommit.Parent(0); err == nil {
    90→			if pTree, err := parent.Tree(); err == nil {
    91→				parentTree = pTree
    92→			}
    93→		}
    94→	}
    95→
    96→	// Check each file in filesTouched
    97→	for _, filePath := range filesTouched {
    98→		// Get file from HEAD tree (the committed content)
    99→		headFile, err := headTree.File(filePath)
   100→		if err != nil {
   101→			// File not in HEAD commit. This happens when:
   102→			// - The session created/modified the file but user deleted it before committing
   103→			// - The file was staged as a deletion (git rm)
   104→			// In both cases, the session's work on this file is not in the commit,
   105→			// so it doesn't contribute to overlap. Continue checking other files.
   106→			continue
   107→		}
   108→
   109→		// Check if this is a modified file (exists in parent) or new file
   110→		isModified := false
   111→		if parentTree != nil {
   112→			if _, err := parentTree.File(filePath); err == nil {
   113→				isModified = true
   114→			}
   115→		}
   116→
   117→		// Modified files always count as overlap (user edited session's work)
   118→		if isModified {
   119→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "filesOverlapWithContent: modified file counts as overlap",
   120→				slog.String("file", filePath),
   121→			)
   122→			return true
   123→		}
   124→
   125→		// For new files, check content against shadow branch
   126→		shadowFile, err := shadowTree.File(filePath)
   127→		if err != nil {
   128→			// File not in shadow branch - this shouldn't happen but skip it
   129→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "filesOverlapWithContent: file in filesTouched but not in shadow branch",
   130→				slog.String("file", filePath),
   131→			)
   132→			continue
   133→		}
   134→
   135→		// Compare by hash (blob hash) - exact content match required for new files
   136→		if headFile.Hash == shadowFile.Hash {
   137→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "filesOverlapWithContent: new file content match found",
   138→				slog.String("file", filePath),
   139→				slog.String("hash", headFile.Hash.String()),
   140→			)
   141→			return true
   142→		}
   143→
   144→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "filesOverlapWithContent: new file content mismatch (may be reverted & replaced)",
   145→			slog.String("file", filePath),

> AGENT

This is revealing. Looking at the data:

1. **All 7 sessions claim `files_touched = ["testenv.go"]`** but the transcripts show they worked on completely different files (agent.go, claudecode/claude.go, etc.)

2. **All sessions have `worktree_id: null` and `base_commit: null`** - this is suspicious, these should be set

3. **The commit was made BEFORE this branch's fixes** - so the old logic was used

The issue is: **before the fix, `filesOverlapWithContent` was called, but NOT as a gate for condensation**. Let me show you the old logic vs new:

**OLD (buggy) logic:**
```go
shouldCondense := len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 && h.hasNew
```
This only checked if `FilesTouched` was non-empty and if there was new content. It didn't check if the session's files overlapped with the committed files.

**NEW (fixed) logic:**
```go
shouldCondense := len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 && h.shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck(...)
```
This now intersects `filesTouchedBefore` with `committedFileSet` before checking content overlap.

The key difference is line 556-563 in the fix:
```go
var committedTouchedFiles []string
for _, f := range h.filesTouchedBefore {
    if _, ok := h.committedFileSet[f]; ok {
        committedTouchedFiles = append(committedTouchedFiles, f)
    }
}
if len(committedTouchedFiles) == 0 {
    return false  // No overlap - don't condense!
}
```

Before this fix, if ALL 7 sessions had `files_touched = ["testenv.go"]` (whether correct or not), and testenv.go was committed, they'd ALL pass the `len(state.FilesTouched) > 0` check. The `filesOverlapWithContent` would return `true` for all of them because testenv.go was a modified file (existed in parent).

The remaining question is: **why did all 7 sessions have `files_touched = ["testenv.go"]` when their transcripts show they worked on different files?** That seems like a separate bug in how `FilesTouched` was being populated or persisted.

> AGENT

Found it! Look at lines 160-168 in `manual_commit_condensation.go`:

```go
// If extraction failed or returned empty, use committedFiles as fallback.
// This handles mid-session commits where transcript parsing may not find files
// but we know what was committed.
if len(sessionData.FilesTouched) == 0 {
    sessionData.FilesTouched = make([]string, 0, len(committedFiles))
    for f := range committedFiles {
        sessionData.FilesTouched = append(sessionData.FilesTouched, f)
    }
}
```

Here's what happened:

1. Sessions 0-5 had `FilesTouched` with files like `agent.go`, `claude.go`, etc. (NOT testenv.go)
2. **OLD code** (before this PR): `shouldCondense = len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 && hasNew` → all sessions passed
3. `CondenseSession` filters `FilesTouched` to intersection with `committedFiles` ({"testenv.go"}) → **empty intersection**
4. **The fallback kicks in**: "if empty, use committedFiles" → gives them `["testenv.go"]`
5. All 7 sessions end up with `files_touched = ["testenv.go"]` in their condensed metadata

This PR's fix adds the overlap check **before** calling `CondenseSession`, so sessions without overlapping files never reach that fallback. But the fallback logic itself is questionable - it assumes an empty intersection means "mid-session commit where we couldn't parse files" rather than "session unrelated to this commit".

Should we also fix the fallback to not silently override with `committedFiles`? Or is the upstream overlap check sufficient now?

> DEVELOPER

yes, I agree we should handle this better, I don't see a reason if there where FilesTouched but the intersection is empty we need a fallback, can you trace back why the fallback was added?

> AGENT

Found it. Commit `45d15515` added the fallback with this rationale:

```
// If extraction failed or returned empty, use committedFiles as fallback.
// This handles mid-session commits where transcript parsing may not find files
// but we know what was committed.
```

The intent was for **mid-session commits** where:
1. Agent is ACTIVE, no Stop/SaveStep called yet
2. `state.FilesTouched` is empty (not populated yet)
3. Transcript parsing fails or returns empty
4. Fall back to `committedFiles` since "we know what was committed"

But the bug is that the fallback **also** triggers when:
1. Session has `FilesTouched = ["agent.go", "claude.go"]` (unrelated files)
2. Filter to intersection with `committedFiles = {"testenv.go"}` → empty
3. Fallback kicks in → wrongly assigns `["testenv.go"]`

The fix should distinguish these cases. Let me check what data is available:

> AGENT

All tests pass and lint is clean. Here's a summary of the fix:

## Summary of Changes

**Root cause of the 7-session checkpoint bug:**

1. Sessions 0-5 had `FilesTouched` with files like `agent.go`, `claude.go` (NOT testenv.go)
2. OLD overlap check let them through to `CondenseSession`
3. Filtering: `intersection(["agent.go"], {"testenv.go"})` = `[]` (empty)
4. **Bug**: Fallback kicked in and assigned `["testenv.go"]` to ALL sessions
5. Result: 7 sessions all with `files_touched = ["testenv.go"]`

**The fix distinguishes two cases:**

| Case | Before Filtering | After Filtering | Action |
|------|-----------------|-----------------|--------|
| Mid-session commit | `[]` (empty) | `[]` | Fallback to `committedFiles` ✓ |
| Unrelated session | `["agent.go"]` | `[]` | Keep empty, don't fallback ✓ |

**Code change:**
```go
hadFilesBeforeFiltering := len(sessionData.FilesTouched) > 0
// ... filtering ...
if len(sessionData.FilesTouched) == 0 && !hadFilesBeforeFiltering {
    // Only fallback for genuine mid-session commits
}
```

This works together with the upstream `shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck` fix - sessions with non-overlapping files shouldn't reach `CondenseSession` at all now, but this provides defense-in-depth to prevent incorrect `files_touched` assignment if they do.

err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to write file: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif _, err := worktree.Add(\"agent.go\"); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to stage file: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif _, err = worktree.Commit(\"Add agent.go\", &git.CommitOptions{\n\t\tAuthor: &object.Signature{Name: \"Agent\", Email: \"agent@test.com\", When: time.Now()},\n\t}); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to commit: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tt.Chdir(dir)\n\n\t// Create live transcript (required when no shadow branch)\n\ttranscriptDir := filepath.Join(dir, \".claude\", \"projects\", \"test\")\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(transcriptDir, 0o755); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to create transcript dir: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\ttranscriptFile := filepath.Join(transcriptDir, \"session.jsonl\")\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(transcriptFile, []byte(`{\"type\":\"human\",\"message\":{\"content\":\"create agent.go\"}}\n{\"type\":\"assistant\",\"message\":{\"content\":\"Done\"}}\n`), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to write transcript: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Session state with FilesTouched that does NOT overlap with committedFiles\n\tstate := &SessionState{\n\t\tSessionID:      \"test-no-overlap\",\n\t\tBaseCommit:     initialHash.String(),\n\t\tFilesTouched:   []string{\"session_file.go\"}, // Does NOT overlap with other_file.go\n\t\tTranscriptPath: transcriptFile,\n\t\tAgentType:      \"Claude Code\",\n\t}\n\n\ts := &ManualCommitStrategy{}\n\tcheckpointID := id.MustCheckpointID(\"nooverlap001\")\n\n\t// Condense with committedFiles that don't overlap\n\tcommittedFiles := map[string]struct{}{\"other_file.go\": {}}\n\tresult, err := s.CondenseSession(repo, checkpointID, state, committedFiles)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"CondenseSession() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Read metadata and verify files_touched is EMPTY (no fallback applied)\n\tsessionsRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to get sessions branch: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tsessionsCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(sessionsRef.Hash())\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to get sessions commit: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\ttree, err := sessionsCommit.Tree()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to get tree: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tmetadataPath := checkpointID.Path() + \"/0/\" + paths.MetadataFileName\n\tmetadataFile, err := tree.File(metadataPath)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to find metadata: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tcontent, err := metadataFile.Contents()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to read metadata: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tvar metadata struct {\n\t\tFilesTouched []string `json:\"files_touched\"`\n\t}\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &metadata); > AGENT

All tests pass and lint is clean. Here's a summary of the changes made in this session:

## Summary of Changes

### 1. Fixed the fallback logic in `manual_commit_condensation.go`

**Problem:** When `state.FilesTouched` had files that didn't overlap with `committedFiles`, the intersection became empty and the fallback incorrectly assigned `committedFiles` to the session's `files_touched`.

**Fix:** Only apply the fallback when `state.FilesTouched` was **empty to begin with** (genuine mid-session commit), not when it had files that simply didn't overlap.

```go
hadFilesBeforeFiltering := len(sessionData.FilesTouched) > 0
// ... filtering ...
if len(sessionData.FilesTouched) == 0 && !hadFilesBeforeFiltering {
    // Only fallback for genuine mid-session commits
}
```

### 2. Added unit tests for both scenarios in `manual_commit_test.go`

| Test | Scenario | Expected Result |
|------|----------|-----------------|
| `TestCondenseSession_FilesTouchedFallback_EmptyState` | `state.FilesTouched = []` (empty) | Fallback to `committedFiles` ✓ |
| `TestCondenseSession_FilesTouchedNoFallback_NoOverlap` | `state.FilesTouched = ["session_file.go"]` but committed `other_file.go` | Keep `files_touched = []`, no fallback ✓ |

### Files Changed
- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_condensation.go` - Fixed fallback logic
- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_test.go` - Added 2 new unit tests

This fix works together with the `shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck` fix to prevent:
1. Sessions being condensed into unrelated commits (upstream check)
2. Sessions getting incorrect `files_touched` if they do reach `CondenseSession` (this fix)

> DEVELOPER

and does it make sense to have this covered with integration tests?

> AGENT

Good question. Let me think through whether integration tests add value here:

**Scenario 1: Empty `FilesTouched`, fallback should work (mid-session commit)**
- This is a legitimate user flow: agent commits before Stop/SaveStep is called
- Integration test would need to: start session → agent commits mid-turn (no Stop) → verify `files_touched` is populated from `committedFiles`
- This could catch bugs in the full flow that unit tests miss

**Scenario 2: No overlap, no fallback**
- With the upstream `shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck` fix, sessions without overlap shouldn't reach `CondenseSession` at all
- The fallback fix is defense-in-depth
- The existing integration tests (`carry_forward_overlap_test.go`) already test the upstream check

My recommendation:
- **Yes** for scenario 1 (mid-session commit) - it's a real user flow worth testing end-to-end
- **No** for scenario 2 - it's defense-in-depth covered by unit tests, and the upstream flow is already integration-tested

Want me to add an integration test for the mid-session commit scenario? It would verify that when an ACTIVE session commits before Stop is called, the checkpoint correctly gets `files_touched` from the committed files.

> DEVELOPER

yes, let's add scenario 1

    32→// testBinaryPath holds the path to the CLI binary built once in TestMain.
    33→// All tests share this binary to avoid repeated builds.
    34→var testBinaryPath string
    35→
    36→// getTestBinary returns the path to the shared test binary.
    37→// It panics if TestMain hasn't run (testBinaryPath is empty).
    38→func getTestBinary() string {
    39→	if testBinaryPath == "" {
    40→		panic("testBinaryPath not set - TestMain must run before tests")
    41→	}
    42→	return testBinaryPath
    43→}
    44→
    45→// TestEnv manages an isolated test environment for integration tests.
    46→type TestEnv struct {
    47→	T                *testing.T
    48→	RepoDir          string
    49→	ClaudeProjectDir string
    50→	GeminiProjectDir string
    51→	SessionCounter   int
    52→}
    53→
    54→// NewTestEnv creates a new isolated test environment.
    55→// It creates temp directories for the git repo and agent project files.
    56→// Note: Does NOT change working directory to allow parallel test execution.
    57→// Note: Does NOT use t.Setenv to allow parallel test execution - CLI commands
    58→// receive the env var via cmd.Env instead.
    59→func NewTestEnv(t *testing.T) *TestEnv {
    60→	t.Helper()
    61→
    62→	// Resolve symlinks on macOS where /var -> /private/var
    63→	// This ensures the CLI subprocess and test use consistent paths
    64→	repoDir := t.TempDir()
    65→	if resolved, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(repoDir); err == nil {
    66→		repoDir = resolved
    67→	}
    68→	claudeProjectDir := t.TempDir()
    69→	if resolved, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(claudeProjectDir); err == nil {
    70→		claudeProjectDir = resolved
    71→	}
    72→	geminiProjectDir := t.TempDir()
    73→	if resolved, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(geminiProjectDir); err == nil {
    74→		geminiProjectDir = resolved
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	env := &TestEnv{
    78→		T:                t,
    79→		RepoDir:          repoDir,
    80→		ClaudeProjectDir: claudeProjectDir,
    81→		GeminiProjectDir: geminiProjectDir,
    82→	}
    83→
    84→	// Note: Don't use t.Setenv here - it's incompatible with t.Parallel()
    85→	// CLI commands receive ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR or ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR via cmd.Env instead
    86→
    87→	return env
    88→}
    89→
    90→// Cleanup is a no-op retained for backwards compatibility.
    91→//
    92→// Previously this method restored the working directory after NewTestEnv changed it.
    93→// With the refactor to remove os.Chdir from NewTestEnv:
    94→// - Temp directories are now cleaned up automatically by t.TempDir()
    95→// - Working directory is never changed, so no restoration is needed
    96→//
    97→// This method is kept to avoid breaking existing tests that call defer env.Cleanup().
    98→// New tests should not call this method as it serves no purpose.
    99→//
   100→// Deprecated: This method is a no-op and will be removed in a future version.

     1→//go:build integration
     2→
     3→package integration
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"bytes"
     7→	"encoding/json"
     8→	"os"
     9→	"os/exec"
    10→	"testing"
    11→
    12→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    13→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy"
    14→)
    15→
    16→// TestShadowStrategy_MidSessionCommit_FromTranscript tests that when Claude commits
    17→// mid-session (before Stop has been called), the prepare-commit-msg hook detects
    18→// the new work by checking the live transcript and adds a checkpoint trailer.
    19→//
    20→// This is scenario 2 from ENT-112:
    21→// - User prompts Claude
    22→// - Claude creates files and commits them
    23→// - No Stop has happened yet (no shadow branch)
    24→// - The commit should still get a checkpoint trailer because the transcript shows file modifications
    25→func TestShadowStrategy_MidSessionCommit_FromTranscript(t *testing.T) {
    26→	t.Parallel()
    27→
    28→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
    29→
    30→	session := env.NewSession()
    31→
    32→	// Simulate user prompt (initializes session with BaseCommit and TranscriptPath)
    33→	// We need to pass the transcript path so it gets stored in session state
    34→	input := map[string]string{
    35→		"session_id":      session.ID,
    36→		"transcript_path": session.TranscriptPath,
    37→	}
    38→	inputJSON, _ := json.Marshal(input)
    39→	cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "claude-code", "user-prompt-submit")
    40→	cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
    41→	cmd.Stdin = bytes.NewReader(inputJSON)
    42→	cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
    43→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+env.ClaudeProjectDir,
    44→	)
    45→	if output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
    46→		t.Fatalf("user-prompt-submit failed: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
    47→	}
    48→
    49→	// Verify session state has transcript path
    50→	state, err := env.GetSessionState(session.ID)
    51→	if err != nil {
    52→		t.Fatalf("Failed to get session state: %v", err)
    53→	}
    54→	if state == nil {
    55→		t.Fatal("Session state is nil")
    56→	}
    57→	if state.TranscriptPath == "" {
    58→		t.Error("Session state should have TranscriptPath after user-prompt-submit")
    59→	}
    60→	t.Logf("Session state TranscriptPath: %s", state.TranscriptPath)
    61→
    62→	// Create a file as if Claude wrote it
    63→	env.WriteFile("claude_file.txt", "content from Claude")
    64→
    65→	// Create transcript showing Claude created the file (NO Stop called)
    66→	session.CreateTranscript("Create a file for me", []FileChange{
    67→		{Path: "claude_file.txt", Content: "content from Claude"},
    68→	})
    69→
    70→	// Verify NO shadow branch exists (Stop hasn't been called)
    71→	shadowBranches := env.ListBranchesWithPrefix("entire/")
    72→	hasShadowBranch := false
    73→	for _, b := range shadowBranches {
    74→		if b != paths.MetadataBranchName {
    75→			hasShadowBranch = true
    76→			break
    77→		}
    78→	}
    79→	if hasShadowBranch {
    80→		t.Error("Shadow branch should not exist before Stop is called")
    81→	}
    82→
    83→	// Get HEAD before commit
    84→	headBefore := env.GetHeadHash()
    85→
    86→	// Commit with shadow hooks - should add trailer because transcript shows file modifications
    87→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add file from Claude (mid-session)", "claude_file.txt")
    88→
    89→	// Get the commit
    90→	commitHash := env.GetHeadHash()
    91→	if commitHash == headBefore {
    92→		t.Fatal("Commit was not created")
    93→	}
    94→
    95→	// CRITICAL: Verify commit has a checkpoint trailer
    96→	// This is the fix for ENT-112 scenario 2: detect work from live transcript
    97→	checkpointID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commitHash)
    98→	if checkpointID == "" {
    99→		t.Error("Mid-session commit should have Entire-Checkpoint trailer when transcript shows file modifications")
   100→	} else {
   101→		t.Logf("Mid-session commit has checkpoint ID: %s", checkpointID)
   102→	}
   103→}
   104→
   105→// TestShadowStrategy_MidSessionCommit_NoTrailerWithoutTranscriptPath tests that
   106→// when TranscriptPath is not set in session state, commits don't get erroneous
   107→// checkpoint trailers (graceful fallback).
   108→func TestShadowStrategy_MidSessionCommit_NoTrailerWithoutTranscriptPath(t *testing.T) {
   109→	t.Parallel()
   110→
   111→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   112→
   113→	session := env.NewSession()
   114→
   115→	// Simulate user prompt WITHOUT transcript path (legacy behavior)
   116→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
   117→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   118→	}
   119→
   120→	// Create a file manually (not through Claude)
   121→	env.WriteFile("manual_file.txt", "manual content")
   122→
   123→	// Don't create transcript - simulating a case where transcript path isn't available
   124→
   125→	// Commit with shadow hooks
   126→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Manual commit without transcript", "manual_file.txt")
   127→
   128→	// Commit should NOT have checkpoint trailer (no session activity detected)
   129→	commitHash := env.GetHeadHash()
   130→	checkpointID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commitHash)
   131→	if checkpointID != "" {
   132→		t.Errorf("Commit without session activity should not have checkpoint trailer, got: %s", checkpointID)
   133→	}
   134→}
   135→
   136→// TestShadowStrategy_MidSessionCommit_NoTrailerForUnrelatedFile tests that
   137→// when Claude has modified files but the committed file is unrelated,
   138→// no checkpoint trailer is added.
   139→func TestShadowStrategy_MidSessionCommit_NoTrailerForUnrelatedFile(t *testing.T) {
   140→	t.Parallel()
   141→
   142→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   143→
   144→	session := env.NewSession()
   145→
   146→	// Simulate user prompt with transcript path
   147→	input := map[string]string{
   148→		"session_id":      session.ID,
   149→		"transcript_path": session.TranscriptPath,
   150→	}
   151→	inputJSON, _ := json.Marshal(input)
   152→	cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "claude-code", "user-prompt-submit")
   153→	cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   154→	cmd.Stdin = bytes.NewReader(inputJSON)
   155→	cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   156→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+env.ClaudeProjectDir,
   157→	)
   158→	if output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   159→		t.Fatalf("user-prompt-submit failed: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
   160→	}
   161→
   162→	// Create transcript showing Claude modified a DIFFERENT file
   163→	session.CreateTranscript("Create a file", []FileChange{
   164→		{Path: "claude_file.txt", Content: "content from Claude"},
   165→	})
   166→
   167→	// Create and commit an UNRELATED file (not in transcript)
   168→	env.WriteFile("unrelated_file.txt", "unrelated content")
   169→
   170→	// Commit with shadow hooks - should NOT add trailer because files don't overlap
   171→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Unrelated file commit", "unrelated_file.txt")
   172→
   173→	commitHash := env.GetHeadHash()
   174→	checkpointID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commitHash)
   175→
   176→	// CRITICAL: No checkpoint trailer should be added for unrelated files
   177→	// The overlap check in sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript ensures this
   178→	if checkpointID != "" {
   179→		t.Errorf("Unrelated file commit should NOT have checkpoint trailer, but got: %s", checkpointID)
   180→	} else {
   181→		t.Log("Correctly omitted checkpoint trailer for unrelated file commit")
   182→	}
   183→}
   184→
   185→// TestShadowStrategy_AgentCommit_AlwaysGetsTrailer tests that when an agent commits
   186→// (ACTIVE session + no TTY), the trailer is always added regardless of content
   187→// detection. This is the fast path that bypasses transcript analysis.
   188→func TestShadowStrategy_AgentCommit_AlwaysGetsTrailer(t *testing.T) {
   189→	t.Parallel()
   190→
   191→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   192→
   193→	session := env.NewSession()
   194→
   195→	// Start session (sets phase to ACTIVE)
   196→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
   197→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   198→	}
   199→
   200→	// Create a file and commit as agent (no TTY)
   201→	env.WriteFile("agent_file.txt", "created by agent")
   202→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooksAsAgent("Agent commit", "agent_file.txt")
   203→
   204→	// Agent commits should ALWAYS get a trailer (fast path, no content detection)
   205→	commitHash := env.GetHeadHash()
   206→	checkpointID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commitHash)
   207→	if checkpointID == "" {
   208→		t.Error("Agent commit during ACTIVE session should always get a checkpoint trailer")
   209→	} else {
   210→		t.Logf("Agent commit correctly got checkpoint trailer: %s", checkpointID)
   211→	}
   212→}
   213→
